## 11.11.2026 (5): Payments and keys audit (owner asked: numbers in the right place, cancellations off the profit, stock back, all keys in environment variables)

- **How:** a runtime audit on the local server with fictional users (scripts outside the repo, `scratchpad/qa/MONEY/`): bank purchase with report and approval, wallet purchase and top-up, monthly account (before/after close and payment), item and whole-order cancels at every stage, coupons, stock, double and parallel submits, and totals across screens and Excel. Core numbers are right everywhere (revenue, net, VAT, cost, wallet, monthly balance, stock; no double counting; lists = reports = lobby = month close = Excel).
- **Fixed (money):**
  - A whole-order cancel request was closed as "approved" (with a "your order was cancelled" email) when the admin refunded only one site. Now it stays open, and the other sites stay on hold, until nothing is left in the order or the request is rejected (`lib/cancel-request.ts`).
  - The order statement PDF printed fixed bank details from the code. It now prints the bank details saved in settings; the fixed ones remain only as a fallback when none are saved (`lib/order-statement.ts`).
  - Cancelling an unpaid item in a coupon order left "מחיר רגיל / חיסכון" wrong (totals were right). Subtotal and discount now follow the item (`lib/account-management.ts`).
  - A ₪0 order (100% coupon) paid by bank transfer was stuck in "awaiting payment" forever (a ₪0 report is refused). It is now settled at once like a wallet purchase (`lib/order-create.ts`).
  - The order confirmation page showed the original totals after a refund; it now shows what is left (`app/order-confirmation.tsx`).
- **Fixed (keys):** the lobby said "email not connected" when the email key lives in the environment (`lib/admin-control-center.ts`); maintenance endpoints compare the automation key in constant time (`route.ts`); each connection card in "חיבורים והגדרות" says where its key is kept (`app/screens.tsx`).
- **Keys, found OK:** no key in the code or in Git history; every integration can read its key from an environment variable (`POSTFLOW_PAYMENTS_SECRET`, `POSTFLOW_EMAIL_SECRET`, `POSTFLOW_WORDPRESS_PASSWORD`, `POSTFLOW_WHATSAPP_SECRET`, `PEXELS_API_KEY`, `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `OPENAI_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY`, plus `SETTINGS_ENCRYPTION_KEY`, `POSTFLOW_AUTOMATION_KEY`, `POSTFLOW_SHEETS_SYNC_KEY`); the payment callback needs a valid signature, a matching amount and is counted once; customer API keys and tokens are stored hashed; Staging carries no live provider keys by design. Production variables could not be read from here (blocked, and Production is not touched).
- **Waiting for the owner's decision (2-4 were decided the same evening, see section 6; 1 is still open):**
  1. Keys typed in the admin panel are saved encrypted in the database (the Gemini form offers this on purpose). Close that option so keys live only in environment variables? Existing stored keys would keep working until moved.
  2. Item cancel on an order with an unverified payment report offers "זיכוי לקרדיט" for the difference (with a warning). The credit is given before the bank was checked and is not taken back if the report is rejected. Recommendation: remove the option; approve the report first, then cancel and credit.
  3. Refund after publication: the supplier cost is removed from profit and from the supplier payable although the article is live (the month Excel footer says the opposite). Should the supplier cost stay?
  4. Writing add-on cost is never recorded, so orders with writing show profit "unknown" and the month profit "not complete". Count writing cost as 0, or add a cost field?
- **Known, not fixed (low or only with card payments on):** supplier payable shows the whole month again after a partial payment (API only); 1-agora difference in "before VAT" after some partial refunds (totals exact); whole cancel of a bank-paid order records the refund only in the activity log and cannot credit the wallet; with card/split payments an unpaid item cancel leaves the card amount stale; a freed stock slot does not resend an article that was blocked for lack of stock; supplier email counter can repeat a number after a cancel.
- **Checks:** fix scripts 15/15 relevant + 8/8, flow scripts 36/36 and 20/20, tsc clean, repo suite: no new failures.
- **Local test data (not in the repo):** fictional orders #1179 and up, users qa-mny-*, coupons MNY7/MNY100/FIX7/FIX100, fictional bank details.
- **לפני העלאה:** nothing temporary in the code. Not deployed; Staging runs 8086a4e.

## 11.11.2026 (6): Owner's decisions after the payments audit, built and checked locally

- **No wallet credit before the bank was checked (decision 2).** Item cancel on an order with a pending payment report no longer offers "זיכוי לקרדיט"; the server refuses it (409) with the reason. Order of work: approve the report, then cancel the item and credit. "תיעוד ידני" and "ללא פעולה" stay (`lib/account-management.ts`, `app/refund-dialog.tsx`).
- **Refund after publication asks about the supplier payment (decision 3).** In the refund window of a published article, once the supplier stop is confirmed, the admin must choose "לא, הספק לא מקבל תשלום" or "כן, משלמים לספק" (required on the server too). Keep: the article is saved with `supplier_cost_kept`, its cost stays in the profit, in the supplier month and payable (`supplierPayableArticle` in `lib/supplier-inventory.ts`, used by `lib/supplier-month.ts`, `lib/admin-suppliers.ts`, `lib/admin-control-center.ts`, `lib/customer-sellers.ts`, `lib/workflow-routes.ts`) and its stock slot stays used. Remove: cost drops from profit and supplier payable, and the stock slot returns. The month Excel note about costs was reworded to match.
- **Writing cost: 10 agorot a word (decision 4).** Saved on the article at purchase (`writing_cost_cents`, `lib/order-create.ts`). Older articles are worked out from the words bought, or from the writing price (15 agorot a word to the customer) when the words were not kept (`writingCost` in `lib/admin-month-report.ts`). Orders with writing no longer show profit "unknown". Writing the customer kept after a publication-only refund still costs (a refund marks the article cancelled, so the parts are read from the refund flags).
- **A freed stock slot sends the waiting article by itself.** `dispatchStockWaiting` (`lib/scheduled-dispatch.ts`): approved articles blocked for lack of stock are sent, oldest first, right after an item refund frees a slot and on every one-minute maintenance tick (covers whole-order cancel, pull-back and a new package). Only sites with a free slot are tried; other delivery problems stay manual.
- **Unpaid order, item removed: the payment link follows.** The cached link is kept only for the amount it was made for; a new link (new idempotency key) is made for what is left, and the amounts already sent out are remembered. A signed payment through an older link for a higher amount settles the order and credits the difference to the wallet; any other amount is refused. Wallet + card orders: the card part shrinks first, the reserved wallet part stays; if nothing is left for the card the order is settled from the reserved wallet part (`app/api/[...path]/route.ts`, `lib/account-management.ts`).
- **Supplier month after a payment:** "to pay" and the stage sums show only what is left (net minus paid), and what was paid stays in "paid"; each row carries `remaining_net` (`lib/admin-suppliers.ts`).
- **Checks:** runtime scripts on the local server: credit rule, supplier question and writing cost 18/18; stock auto-send, supplier remaining, card and wallet+card amounts with signed callbacks 16/16; zero card part and payment through an older link 4/4; refund window in the browser (question shows, no page errors); flow scripts 36/36 and 20/20, click-through 24/24, earlier fix scripts 8/8. Repo tests: `tests/split-payment.mjs` passes again (123 checks; updated to the customer-cancel-is-a-request rule, with new checks for the remade link against its fake gateway), `tests/admin-order-report.mjs` and `tests/orders-finance.mjs` updated to the new rules and stop at the same old assertions as before; full suite: no new failures, one fewer old failure; tsc and the three Railway build tests pass.
- **Not checked against a real payment provider** (none is connected): only the fake gateway of the repo test and signed local callbacks.
- **Still open for the owner:** close the option to type a key in the admin panel (keys only in environment variables)? Not answered yet, not changed.
- **Known, not fixed (low):** 1-agora difference in "before VAT" after some partial refunds (totals exact); whole cancel of a bank-paid order records the refund only in the activity log and cannot credit the wallet; supplier email counter can repeat a number after a cancel; a second real payment on an already paid order is not refunded automatically.
- **לפני העלאה:** nothing temporary in the code. Not deployed; Staging runs 8086a4e. Local only: test payment connection was switched on for the checks and removed again; fictional orders, coupons FIX7/FIX100, fictional bank details.

## 12.11.2026 (9): Marketing mailing base (owner: "build the base and the place in admin to take the emails out, and the customer's opt-in buttons; the email design comes later; what the law requires, short and tidy")

- **Decided:** email only, sent from an external mailing tool (owner, option B). The site keeps who agreed, the admin exports the list before each send and pastes back who unsubscribed there. No sending from the site itself.
- **Consent store:** `lib/marketing-consent.ts`, one record per user `marketing:<user id>` (kind `marketing_consent`, no schema change): status, consent date, exact wording shown (`lib/marketing-wording.ts`), source (checkout / account / admin), unsubscribe date, last 20 changes; every change is in the activity log. Saving the same state again keeps the first consent date.
- **Checkout (`app/purchase-flow.tsx`, `app/checkout-consents.css`):** two equal boxes side by side (stacked on a phone): order approval (new wording without "קראתי": terms, cancellations, supply, order details) and the newsletter box (unticked, "לא חובה. אפשר להסיר בכל עת."). A customer who already subscribed sees a green "אתם רשומים..." box instead. Only a ticked box subscribes (`b.marketing===true` in the checkout route, after the order is created); an empty box never unsubscribes. Short privacy line under the order button with a link to /privacy.

## 12.11.2026 (10): Agency centre ("הלקוחות שלי" for agencies): spec and mockups

- **Owner's request (night of 11-12.11, started at 04:00 as he asked):** replace the thin "הלקוחות שלי" with a full dashboard for authorised customers to run their whole agency: customers list on the right, per-customer screen with details / publications (filter by month, year) / articles they can handle and send like regular articles (same records, no double counting), reports, a page to paste a long list of sites already used for a customer (hidden or alert when buying for them), more useful pages, light and clean design with the logo and the installed design skills, everything connected to admin. Spec first, then build.
- **Spec:** `design/agency-dashboard/spec.md` (Hebrew). Mapped from the code: customers are `records` kind `customer`; assignment `article_customer_assignment` (+ `customer_id` in the cart brief); role `agency` already exists in admin users (today only a label) and is proposed as the permission; article money in `data` (`paid_net_amount`, `publication_price`, refund flags). Pages: overview, customers (tabs: overview, details, publications, articles, used sites, links and anchors, notes), all publications, to-do, publication calendar, buy for a customer (catalog customer mode), reports, agency settings. 11 build phases. Open decisions for the owner in section 17.

- **Built, phases 1-3 (12.11.2026, local only):** agency permission on the server (`assertAgency`, role `agency`), paths `/purchased/agency[/customers|/publications]`, `/purchased/agency/customers/<id>[/details|/publications|/articles]` (`lib/seo.ts`, checked before the older 4-part rule), the full-page centre (`app/agency-center.tsx` + `.css`, mounted from `app/client-hub.tsx` when part is `agency`; an agency on `purchased/customers` is sent to it; the tab is called "מרכז הסוכנות" for agencies, also in the phone menu). Customers list on the right (search incl. domains, active / archive / all, waiting-article badge), customer screen with tabs publications (quick ranges, year/month/status, summary, table, phone cards, CSV export), articles (by stage, open in the existing editor) and details (extended fields: contact, domains normalized, tags, monthly budget; archive). All-publications page with a customer filter incl. "ללא לקוח". Server: `lib/agency.ts` (+ pure helpers `lib/agency-shared.ts`), routes `GET/POST agency/customers`, `GET/PATCH agency/customers/<id>`, `GET agency/customers/<id>/articles`, `GET agency/publications`. Spend = paid before VAT minus refunded parts; unpaid orders are not publications.
- **Checks:** `tests/agency-centre.mjs` (new: paths, domain normalization, spend). Local scripts (`scratchpad/qa/AGENCY/`): API 29/29 (permissions, other accounts, validation, customers + no customer = all, filters, no cost/supplier leak, page refresh on every path), browser 31/31 (desktop + phone, redirect of a regular customer, open customer, filters, CSV, details save, no horizontal scroll, no page errors). Regular customers' "הלקוחות שלי" is unchanged.

- **Next phases:** used sites (5), buy for a customer (6), overview + to-do (7), calendar (8), reports + settings (9), links + notes (10), admin connection (11).
- **Built, phase 5 (used sites):** customer tab "אתרים שכבר שימשו" (`UsedSites` in `app/agency-center.tsx`). Sources: automatic (articles of the customer that are published or sent to the site) and a pasted / uploaded (CSV, TXT) list, up to 5,000, stored in `records` kind `customer_used_sites` id `used-sites:<customer>`. Matching (`lib/agency-shared.ts`): domain without www, scheme, port; an entry with a first-level section (e.g. ynet.co.il/capital) covers that section only, a bare domain covers all its sections; article paths (/article/123, /item/..) count as the site. Mode per customer: hide (default) or show with a label. **Server enforcement:** adding to the cart for a customer of an agency a site already used for that customer is refused with 409 `USED_SITE:{...}` unless `confirm_used_site: true`; the confirmation is kept on the item (`used_site_confirmed`) and an edit of that item is not asked again. Applies to single add, bundles, bulk orders and cart edits (all go through `validBrief`). Checks: repo test (matching, parsing) + API 16/16 + browser 13/13.

## 12.11.2026 (14): Phone pass on the customer management centre (owner: "something there does not work out and is not clear")

Since then those screens had no layout on phone **and on desktop** (calendar as a list of numbers, notes and links as plain text). The click tests still passed because they checked behaviour, not look. **This broken version is what Staging runs now (879a54b).** Restored the block from git history at its old place.

Local script `p16` checks layout on phone and desktop (57 checks: 7-column calendar, cards styled, every control on screen, no horizontal scroll, current tab in view).
- **Phone improvements (CSS in `app/agency-center.css`, small hooks in `app/agency-center.tsx`):**
  - Main tabs and customer tabs still scroll sideways, but the side that hides more now fades and the current tab is brought into view (`useScrollHint`).
  - Filters no longer scroll sideways: the quick ranges sit three in a row, the selects two in a row and "ייצוא CSV" beside them, so nothing hides off the side (the selects and the export button used to be off screen). The calendar's customer select wraps under the month.

  - Article rows: status and "לכתבה" get their own line instead of squeezing the text into three lines.
  - "רכישה עבור הלקוח" is full width on the customer screen.
  - The "show amounts" checkbox in settings was drawn as a tall text field (form input rule); fixed on phone and desktop.

## 12.11.2026 (15): Security check before the Staging deploy (owner: no hole in payments or accounts; the centre is only a sorting tool, payments stay on the same path)

- **How the centre is built (checked in code):** `lib/agency.ts` writes only to `records` (customers' extended details, notes, used-sites lists, settings). It has no code touching orders, payments, wallet, refunds or the monthly account. "רכישה עבור הלקוח" only adds `customer_id` to the cart item; cart, quote, checkout, payment and invoice are the regular ones. Everything the centre shows is read from the account's own orders and articles.
- **Attack attempts on the local server** (`scratchpad/qa/AGENCY/sec.mjs`, 73 checks, all refused): reading / changing another account's customers, notes, used-sites lists and publications in both directions; another account's note through my own customer; the admin view for a customer or supplier, with a write method, towards `me` / `orders` / `cart` / `wallet`, and for an admin or supplier account; forged money fields on a cart item (price, total, discount, paid, on_account, owner) do not move the quote; buying for a customer costs exactly the same; another account's customer cannot be attached to a cart item; another account's file cannot become my logo (and cannot be read: 403). Every publication in the centre is one of the account's own articles and orders, with the same status and customer as in "האתרים שלי", and never shows more than was paid.
- **Independent code review (a separate agent, read only) of everything since 8086a4e:** no way for one account to reach another's data, no way for a client to change a price, total, credit or paid status. Findings:
  1. **Medium, payments, NOT fixed yet (owner's decision):** a card payment that arrives for an order that is already paid is answered "ok" and dropped: nothing is credited and nobody is alerted. Verified in code: `completeOrderPayment` (`lib/split-payment.ts`) returns `duplicate` on a paid order before the surplus credit runs, and the callback (`route.ts`, payment callback) then logs "תשלום אומת". Happens when an old payment link is paid after the order was settled from the wallet alone, or when two links of the same order are both paid. The customer loses the money until someone notices. Not part of the agency centre; it is in the payment-link code from 11.11. Staging has no live payment provider, Production is not touched.
  2. Low, fixed: the admin view logged only the first request; now every section opened is logged, once per section in ten minutes (`route.ts`, `admin/agency-view`).
  3. Low, fixed: the centre trusted the stored customer JSON (an older route stores it as sent); the row's id and dates now always win and text / list fields are coerced (`parseRecord` in `lib/agency.ts`).
  4. Low, left as is: the used-site confirmation is the buyer's own warning, not a permission.
  5. Low, fixed: matching a long used-sites list against the catalog parsed every site's address for every entry; each site's key is now worked out once (3,000 entries answer in about 45ms locally).
- **Checks:** sec 73, sec2 9, p16 57 and all earlier agency scripts pass; `tsc --noEmit`; repo tests agency-centre, cart-checkout-actions, checkout-phone-actions, cart-add-feedback, customer-account-actions, seo, bulk-order.
- **Local test leftovers (local DB only):** archived fictional customers "לקוח רשומה משובשת" (qa-cust-a) and "בדיקת עומס זמנית" (qa-cust-b).
- **לפני העלאה:** nothing temporary in the code.

## 12.11.2026 (16): Login screen with Apple / Google and a terms approval step: mockup only (owner's request)

- **Asked:** a preparation for the login screen: sign in with Apple or with Google, then a screen where a new customer approves the terms of use to register, with a checkbox there. Designed with the installed skills (signup flow, Apple-style design).
- **Today:** the app has no login screen of its own. Identity comes from the hosting platform's headers (`actor()` in `lib/server.ts`); Staging has its own email + password page in `scripts/railway-staging-gateway.mjs`.
- **Mockup (not built, nothing in the app changed):** `design/login-screen/mockup.html` (`?s=login`, `?s=terms`, `?s=ready`) and four images next to it. Screen 1: "כניסה לחשבון" with "כניסה עם Apple", "כניסה עם Google", and below "כניסה עם מייל וסיסמה" (kept so today's accounts are not locked out), plus a line on what we receive from Apple / Google. Screen 2, first sign-in only: the account that signed in, a required box for the terms of use and the privacy policy (starts empty; the button is off until it is ticked), an optional box for marketing mail (the existing marketing consent), "פתיחת החשבון". The time of approval and the terms version are to be saved.
- **Needed from the owner before building:** approval of the design; a Google Cloud project (OAuth client) and an Apple Developer account (Sign in with Apple needs the paid developer programme); the site's final address for the return links; whether email + password stays as a third way.
- **Open for the lawyer:** wording of the approval line; the terms box must not come pre-ticked.
- **Build plan written (by a research agent, no code changed):** `design/login-screen/plan.md`: owner summary first, then developer detail. Facts found: `actor()` trusts the `oai-authenticated-user-*` headers (`app/chatgpt-auth.ts`); the app itself has no session, cookie or password; users are matched by id and `users.email` is unique; admin rights follow the email alone (`POSTFLOW_OWNER_EMAIL`, `POSTFLOW_ADMIN_EMAILS`), so only an email verified by Google / Apple may be trusted; email + password exists only in the Staging gateway, so the mockup's third button "כניסה עם מייל וסיסמה" would be a separate, large piece of work for Production (my earlier note that it keeps today's accounts from being locked out is true for Staging only). Could not be determined from the code: whether Production's hosting allows public access with our own login. Google's documentation hosts were blocked in the sandbox; unconfirmed points are marked "לא אומת" in the plan.

Budget (existing field, before VAT, against `stats.month_spend`): a line with a bar under the customer's name on every tab; from 80% amber "קרוב לתקציב החודשי", from 100% red "חריגה מהתקציב החודשי"; a marker in the customers list from 80%; a task per customer in "לטיפול" and "לטיפול עכשיו"; same in the admin's read-only view. No new field is accepted by the server and the price is the same. Admin tag "אתר שכבר שומש ללקוח · אושר בהזמנה" on the item in the order panel; I added it to the full order page too (`app/admin-order-approved.tsx`, `app/orders-approved.css`), where it was missing. `used_site_confirmed` already reached the article data.

Catalog numbers used in the text: 1,186 active sites, 18 categories, 248 local news sites (`data/catalog.json`).

The mockup's customer is fictional; site names and prices are real catalog rows (מיינט חיפה ₪450, ארץ הקפה ₪250, פורטל הכרמל והצפון ₪150).

## 14.11.2026 (20): Staging deploy confirmed; AI question on the idea

- **Staging:** the owner deployed from the Railway dashboard on 13.11 (deployment `3211b72a`, commit `82004bc`, which holds all app code up to `f9500fb` plus design files). Status SUCCESS, server "Ready", no error lines in the deploy log. Production not touched.
- **Owner asked whether the website-plan idea needs a permanent AI connection.** Answer: no. The system already has an AI connection for "יצירת טיוטה בעזרת AI" (Gemini / OpenAI / Groq / Grok keys from environment variables, `lib/integration-security.ts`, `lib/server.ts`); the plan would call it only when a customer asks for a plan, once per plan. A first version can work with no AI at all: read the site's title, description and headings, find the city from a list of Israeli city names and the field from keyword lists mapped to catalog categories, pick sites by city and category within the budget, and use ready topic templates. AI only improves topics and anchors, and the plan falls back to the simple version when AI is unavailable.

## 14.11.2026 (21): "תוכנית פרסום מהאתר שלכם", base version built (owner: "תבנה בסיס לזה"), no AI

- **What it does:** page `/site-plan`. The customer enters their website address; the server reads the home page and up to 5 more pages of the same site (menu / services / products first, then about and contact), finds the business name, the field (catalog category + a short term such as "בית קפה") and the city / region, and builds a plan in three sizes (חסכונית up to ₪300 a site, מאוזנת up to ₪700, חזקה up to ₪3,000): a local site in the city, a site in the field, a regional / national / general site as fallback; each with "למה", a topic from templates, a different page of the customer's site and a different anchor. The customer can swap a site (next candidates), edit topic / link / anchor, remove a site, switch size, and add the plan to the regular cart (`addToCartChecked`, same route, same prices; the brand is the business name). Buying for an agency customer: the plan is for that customer, its domain is filled in, its sites already used are left out, and its brief defaults fill the brief.
- **Files:** `lib/site-plan.ts` (pure: address check, private-address check, page reading, pages to read, field and city lists, business name, plan building), `lib/site-plan-read.ts` (server: safe reading and `sitePlan`), route `POST /api/site-plan` in `app/api/[...path]/route.ts`, `app/site-plan.tsx` + `.css` (from the approved mockup), routed in `app/workspace.tsx`, path allowed in `lib/seo.ts`, test `tests/site-plan.mjs` with fixtures in `tests/fixtures/site-plan/`.
- **Safety:** only public http(s) host names (no IP, no internal names, no password, default ports); every address the name points to must be public (checked with DNS over HTTPS at cloudflare-dns.com, also after each redirect, max 3 redirects); 7 s and 700 KB per page, HTML only; customers and agencies (and admin) only; 20 plans an hour per account; prices from the customer's own pricing; the answer has no cost or supplier fields; nothing from the pages is stored, only a small summary per request (`records` kind `site_plan`, not readable through `/api/records`). Production on Cloudflare also needs outbound access to cloudflare-dns.com and the customers' sites (normal for Workers).
- **Checks:** `tests/site-plan.mjs` 134 checks (pure logic on a fictional café site and the real catalog: name, field, city, targets, three sizes, no repeated site / anchor / topic, catalog prices, links only to the customer's site, no city → no local sites, left-out sites never offered; the built server with stubbed DNS and site: reads the site, customer prices, only DNS and the customer's site fetched, internal address / private DNS answer / redirect to an internal address / missing site / PDF / empty / not signed in refused, another account's customer refused, plans not readable, a plan item goes into the cart and the quote as usual, 21st plan in an hour refused). Browser `scratchpad/qa/AGENCY/p18.mjs` 46/46 on phone and desktop (the local server cannot reach outside sites, so the plan answer was served from the real logic with this customer's real catalog prices; the real route's refusal and error message were checked live): title, empty address, server error shown, reading steps while waiting, plan with city and field, three sizes, totals, swap, edit (16px fields), size switch keeps edits, remove, add to the real cart with topic / link / anchor, regular quote, no horizontal scroll, no page errors. All earlier scripts and repo tests pass.
- **Not done yet:** no entry point in the site (only the address `/site-plan`), waiting for the owner on where to put the button; AI topics (later upgrade); site logos on the plan cards (name tile for now); "תוכנית לחודש הבא" in the agency centre.
- **לפני העלאה:** nothing temporary in the code. Local only; not on Staging.
- **Desktop layout (owner asked how it looks on a computer):** from 980px the start card puts the explanation beside the address field, the plan header puts the size switch beside the title, the plan's sites sit side by side (three cards in a row, actions at the bottom of each card), and the summary is one line (count and total, the checks, the button). Phone layout unchanged. p18 46/46 again on phone and desktop.
- **Entry points proposed (owner asked where the button will be and the exact flow, with marketing and Apple-design advice), not built:** walkthrough boards `design/idea-site-plan/entry-flow-phone.png` and `entry-flow-desk.png`, made from the real pages with the proposed buttons injected only for the screenshots (`scratchpad/qa/AGENCY/entry-shots.mjs`, `storyboard.mjs`). (1) In the catalog hero, under "איפה נפרסם את הכתבה הבאה שלך?", a second, quiet glass button "תוכנית מוכנה מהאתר שלכם" beside the existing "לצפייה בקטלוג" (which stays the main button), with the line "לא בטוחים מה לבחור? מדביקים את כתובת האתר ומקבלים תוכנית בדקה." (2) Above the site list ("אתרים לפרסום"), a light strip "לא יודעים מה לבחור?" with the address field inside it, which goes straight to the plan. (3-8) address, "הכנת התוכנית", size, swap / edit / remove, "הוספת התוכנית לסל", the regular cart and payment. (A) Agency centre, customer screen: "תוכנית לחודש הבא" under "רכישה עבור הלקוח". A visitor who is not signed in signs in with Google and returns to the address field. Reasoning (cro + apple-design skills): the hero question is exactly what the plan answers (message match); one main action stays primary and the plan is the secondary path for undecided visitors; the strip catches people who scrolled into 1,186 sites; the field inside the strip saves a step; quiet tinted button so it does not compete with the main one.
- **Entry point built where the owner asked** ("על הפנים המיקום! תסדר אותו איפשהו איפה הקטגוריות וכו"): the hero button and the strip above the list were dropped. "תוכנית מהאתר שלכם" is now a button among the catalog tools (`app/catalog-view.tsx`, first in `.catalog-results-actions`, class `catalog-plan-trigger`, hidden for admin; not signed in → Google sign-in and back to `/site-plan`). Look: same size as the tools beside it, light blue tint and the brand blue (`app/account-navigation-refinements.css`). Phone: its own full row right under "בחירת קטגוריות" / "סינון ומיון"; the three tools and "הוספת אתר לקטלוג" moved one row down (`app/catalog-filter-apple.css`). From 1180px the five tools share one row (each a fifth), "הוספת אתר לקטלוג" stays alone below as before; 701-1179px three per row. Checked on phone, tablet and desktop: visible, at least 44px high, no horizontal scroll, opens `/site-plan`, admin has no button, no page errors. Repo tests catalog-home-actions, catalog-home-layout, bundle-feedback, seller-refinements-ui pass; catalog-controls-browser ("mobile category w unchanged"), azure-workspace-actions and pricing-redesign-actions (CSS asset "/brand/checkout-waves.webp" not resolved) fail the same way without this change.
- **Not built yet:** "תוכנית לחודש הבא" in the agency customer screen (waiting for the owner).
- **Parked (owner: "לא אהבתי. אל תכניס בינתיים, שמור את התוכנית הזאת כתובה ובהמשך נמצא לה כפתור"):** the whole feature is out of the site again: the catalog button, the `/site-plan` screen, the `POST /api/site-plan` route and its files are removed, and the touched files are back to their state before `a39b56d` (route.ts, workspace.tsx, lib/seo.ts, catalog-view.tsx, account-navigation-refinements.css, catalog-filter-apple.css). Nothing of it will reach Staging. The code stays in this branch's history: `a39b56d` (base) and `eaaa634` (desktop layout); restore with `git cherry-pick a39b56d eaaa634`, then `pnpm build` and `node tests/site-plan.mjs`. Status, what was built, tried buttons and how to restore are written in `design/idea-site-plan/idea.md`. The design files (mockup, walkthrough boards) stay.

## דף בית חדש בכתובת הראשית (15.11.2026, באישור הבעלים)
- **ענף:** `feature/homepage-staging-20261115`, שיוצא מ-`82004bc` (הגרסה שרצה ב-Staging מ-13.11, מהענף `claude/order-flow-qa-20261111`). גרסה קודמת של אותו שינוי, על בסיס ישן, נשארה ב-`feature/homepage-20261115` ונדחפה גם ל-`staging/all-20261108`. לא לפרוס אותה: חסרים בה 68 commits של מרכז הסוכנויות ומסך הכניסה.
- **מה הוחלט ולמה:**
  - דף הבית החדש הוא המסך הראשון של האתר, בכתובת `/`. הבעלים בחר שהקטלוג יעבור לכתובת משלו, `/catalog`, בלי שינוי בתוכן שלו.
  - דף הבית הוא מסמך עצמאי (HTML, CSS ו-JS משלו), ולכן הוא לא נוגע באף מסך קיים ולא מושפע מהעיצוב של המערכת.
  - שלושת כרטיסי "ידע מקצועי בחינם" מוחלפים בשרת בשלושת המדריכים האחרונים שפורסמו. אם אין מדריכים, נשארים כרטיסי הדוגמה.
- **קבצים:**
  - `home/index.html`: המקור של דף הבית. אחרי כל עריכה להריץ `node scripts/build-home-page.mjs`, שמייצר את `lib/home-page-html.ts`.
  - `app/route.ts`: מגיש את דף הבית ב-`/`. `app/page.tsx` נמחק.
  - `app/[...page]/page.tsx`: `/catalog` מציג את הקטלוג (קודם הפנה ל-`/`).
  - `lib/seo.ts`: `/catalog` נוסף לעמודים הציבוריים, והכתובת הקנונית של הקטלוג היא `/catalog`.
  - קישורים לקטלוג עודכנו ב-`workspace.tsx`, `workspace-navigation.tsx`, `workspace-footer.tsx`, `admin-shell.tsx`, `knowledge-shell.tsx`, `knowledge-components.tsx`.
  - `public/homepage/`: התמונות ומנוע הגלילה של דף הבית (scrollcraft, רישיון MIT).
  - בדיקות: `tests/seo.mjs`, `tests/railway-staging-assets.mjs`, `tests/integration.mjs` עודכנו לכתובות החדשות.
- **מקור העיצוב:** ההדמיה נמצאת מחוץ לפרויקט, ב-`C:/Users/User/megalinks-tools/scrollcraft/builds/megalinks-home/index.html`. הסקריפט `C:/Users/User/megalinks-tools/home-to-repo.py` מעתיק אותה לפרויקט ומחבר את הקישורים לעמודי האתר.
- **לא גמור:**
  - הכפתור "לכתבות יח״צ" עדיין לא מוביל לעמוד. הוא מציג הודעה "יעלה בקרוב" עד שייבנה עמוד יח״צ.

## 16.11.2026: Outgoing emails in order, files that really arrive, updated first screen

Owner's request: go over every outgoing email, make the supplier email orderly and with the files themselves (his test email arrived messy and without a file), use RTL, then deploy to Staging. Also: the first screen of the homepage as approved in the mockup.

### What was found
- Emails leave through a webhook. On Staging the webhook is a Zapier hook (seen in the service's DNS log, `hooks.zapier.com`). The Zap itself is the owner's and is not in the repo.
- **No file:** files were sent only as base64 inside the JSON. Zapier's Gmail step cannot turn base64 into an attachment; it needs a file address.
- **Messy:** only 3 of about 24 emails had HTML; the rest were plain text and their look depended on the Zap. The HTML ones set the direction only on `<html>`, which mail clients drop, and used font weight 800, which on Windows falls back to a font without Hebrew.
- **Staging only:** the Staging login wall also blocks the logo image and any file address, so on Staging the logo is missing in emails and Zapier cannot fetch files. Not changed (see "Open").

### What changed
- `lib/mail-template.ts` (new): one right-to-left look for every email (white card, dark logo, headline = subject, text in order, files, one button). The text is split into paragraphs, "label: value" rows and numbered lists. Direction on every block, inline styles, addresses and amounts isolated as left-to-right, bold 700.
- `lib/mail.ts`: every email now goes out with HTML (its own design when it has one, the standard one otherwise). `mailFiles(job)` builds the files of an email in one place. Each file is attached as before **and** gets a personal download address `/api/mail-file/<token>/<n>/<name>` (token stored on the mail job, 45 days, closed if the article was pulled back from the supplier). The payload has new fields `attachment_urls` and `attachment_names`. Above 6MB of files the base64 copies are left out (the service takes about 10MB) and only the addresses travel.
- `app/api/[...path]/route.ts`: public route `mail-file/<token>/<n>/<name>` (GET, download headers, 404 for a wrong token or number); `sendPackageStatus` gets the site address.
- `lib/supplier-mail-content.ts`: the supplier email lists the files with a "הורדה" button each, shows the link(s) the article must carry (anchor and address), direction on every block.
- `lib/order-close-email.ts`, `lib/writing-mail.ts`: same designs, direction fixed, a place for the files block.
- Wording so each detail gets its own row: monthly statement (`lib/monthly.ts`), new top-up request (`lib/finance.ts`), package status (`lib/supplier-orders.ts`), seller invoice request list (`lib/customer-sellers.ts`). Supplier-paid and published emails now carry the site address for the logo (`lib/workflow-routes.ts`, `lib/customer-sellers.ts`, `articleMail`).
- Homepage first screen (`home/index.html`, `lib/home-page-html.ts`, `public/homepage/city-m.webp`): no explanation sentence, original colours, white main button, the search pill is a link to `/catalog`, lighter phone background, and on phones the next screen peeks as a soft arc with a round arrow button.
- Tests: `tests/supplier-mail.mjs` (links, plain emails as RTL pages, escaping), `tests/writing-workflow.mjs` (the download address returns the real PDF without signing in; wrong number or token refused). README email contract updated.

### Checked locally (fictional data, nothing was sent)
- All 23 email kinds were built and rendered the way a mail client shows them, at 600 and 390 wide: no horizontal overflow, every one right-to-left. Supplier email: 4 files, each with its own address.
- `tsc --noEmit` clean, build passes. Passing: supplier-mail, writing-workflow (130), seo (67), railway-staging-assets, payment-audit (120), security-audit (39), monthly-order-lifecycle (90), split-payment (123), pricing-integration (149).
- Failing, not in email code and not checked against the previous build: `tests/orders-finance.mjs` ("combined orders view preserves final approval month") and `tests/customer-account.mjs` (cancel after a refund returns 409; the rule was added on 11.11, the test is from 28.10).
- Run Miniflare tests with the local QA server stopped; otherwise they hang.

### Open (needs the owner)
- **The Zap:** in the Gmail step the body must be the `html` field with body type HTML, and "Attachments" must be `attachment_urls`. Until then the email shows the download buttons but Gmail attaches nothing.
- **Staging wall:** the question whether to let the logo files and `/api/mail-file/` through the Staging login was dismissed without an answer, so the wall is unchanged. On Staging: no logo in emails, download buttons work only for someone signed in to Staging, and Zapier cannot attach. The live site has no such wall in this repo's gateway (it is the Staging gateway script).
- The cloud branch `claude/order-flow-qa-20261111` does not have this work yet.

### לפני העלאה

The system posts JSON (`to, subject, text, html?, button, attachments`) with `Authorization: Bearer`, needs a direct 2xx (redirects are refused, no query strings in the URL). Found: most mails are text + button only (looks bare); designed HTML exists only for supplier article, writing ready and order close. Proposed a shared mail template (not built, waiting). The Apps Script adds only columns missing under any name (`sheetHeaderGroups`).

Two names for one e-mail stop the sync (`lib/sheets-sync.ts`). Typing a sale price turns the sale on unless "במבצע" changes to לא in the same edit (the sync writes "לא" in every row, so an unchanged "לא" does not block). ## 16.11.2026 (23): Resend as the email service, trial first (branch `claude/resend-email-20261116`, from 893089a+notes)
- **Owner:** "אני רוצה להתחיל אבל בניסיון נטו לראות איך מגיעים המיילים, אחר כך לעבוד על שאר הדברים ועל החיבור המוסדר". Asked whether it works on today's code: no, the webhook format has no sender and Resend needs its own; only through Zapier (Zapier's Resend app) without a code change. Went on with a direct connection.
- **Built:** "חיבורים והגדרות → דוא״ל" now has "שירות הדיוור": Webhook (unchanged default) or Resend. Resend fields: sender (default `Mega Links <onboarding@resend.dev>`, Resend's trial sender), optional test inbox, API key (encrypted on the server; on Staging the environment-variable option does not reach the Worker). With a test inbox every message goes only there and the subject starts with "[בדיקה · אל: <real recipient>]". Sends Resend's format (`from,to[],subject,html,text,attachments[{filename,content}]`) with `Idempotency-Key`; 401/403/422/429 become Hebrew hints in the mail log (`lib/mail.ts`, `lib/integration-security.ts`, `app/screens.tsx`). Address fields are left-to-right.
- **Trial limits to remember:** without a verified domain Resend sends only to the address the account was opened with, so the test inbox must be that address. Free plan about 3,000 mails a month, 100 a day (third-party sources). Before real use: verify the domain (DNS records), set the sender to the domain, empty the test inbox.
- **Tests:** new `tests/resend-email.mjs` (26 checks, local stub, never calls Resend). Screen checked on desktop and phone.
- **Deployed to Staging 16.11.2026 (owner: "תעלה"; the first approval prompt was declined, the second approved):** deployment 2435d3c5, SUCCESS, Staging follows `claude/resend-email-20261116` pinned to 290c77e. Before deploying, checked that no branch on GitHub had moved; `feature/homepage-20261115` holds the same homepage on an older base (adapted copy already included). Next deploy must start from 290c77e or later.

## 16.11.2026 (25): Supplier mail files with Resend (owner: "הקובץ לא הגיע במייל עצמו... והתמונה לא הגיעה בנפרד")
- **Cause:** files were attached only up to 6MB in total (set for webhook services, about 10MB a message); the owner's Word file was 8.5MB, so it went as a link. Images and the original document were read only up to 6MB/8MB and were dropped silently above that (no attachment, no link).
- **Fix (`lib/mail.ts`):** with Resend up to 15MB of files are attached (Resend takes 40MB, Gmail receives 25MB); webhook keeps 6MB. Every single file up to 25MB is offered, attached or as a link, so nothing disappears. Download links build the file only when clicked, which costs next to nothing.
- **Tests:** `tests/resend-email.mjs` now 32 checks: a 4MB image arrives attached next to the Word file; with a 12MB image nothing is attached but both files get links.
- Seen, not changed: a broken image file makes the Word builder fail and the whole supplier mail fails (generic error). Real uploads are checked images, so left as is.

## 16.11.2026 (32): Sheets through a Google service account
- **Why:** Google blocks the owner's account from authorising the Apps Script that posts to the site (31). Now the site itself reads and writes the sheet through the Sheets API v4 with a service account. No script in the sheet, no consent screen. The Apps Script push stays as the alternative and works unchanged.
- **How it works:** `lib/sheets-google.ts` signs a JWT (RS256, WebCrypto, no new package), gets a token from `oauth2.googleapis.com/token` (scope spreadsheets, kept in memory until 2 minutes before expiry), then does what `megaLinksSend_` did in the script: adds missing header columns (`sheetHeaderGroups`), reads the tab, runs the same core, writes the cells/new rows back, sends the ack. The push handler was split: `receiveSheets` (auth, size, parse) -> `ingestSheets(input)` (the old body). The pull calls `ingestSheets` with protocol 2, so planning, conflicts, history, price warnings, needs_review, ack digest and writeback_error are the same code.
- **Same protections as the script:** headers changed during the sync, rows added during the sync, a cell changed before writing (checked on a fresh read before the history write and again before the cell write), formula in an edited cell (never overwritten), log to the tab "היסטוריית סנכרון" (created right-to-left with a frozen first row), `USER_ENTERED` with the script's `megaLinksSafeCell_` escaping (`safeCell`), the "מינימום ₪" formula on appended rows. The grid is grown with `appendDimension` before writing past it.
- **Reading:** two reads per pass, `UNFORMATTED_VALUE` (dates as text) and `FORMULA` (dates as serial numbers); a full rectangle like `getValues()`. A date cell becomes yyyy-MM-dd from its serial number. Difference from the script: the script formats the Date in UTC, which can move a date one day back in Israel; here the cell's own date is kept.
- **Key:** pasted in the admin, checked (JSON, `type: service_account`, `client_email` ending in gserviceaccount.com, `private_key` PEM that WebCrypto can import), stored in setting `sheets_service_account` with the private key encrypted by `encrypt()` (SETTINGS_ENCRYPTION_KEY, like the other `encrypted_server` secrets). The browser only gets `client_email`, `key_stored`, `saved_at`, `project_id`. The token URL is fixed in code; `token_uri` from the file is ignored.
- **Sheet id:** `sheets_sync_config.spreadsheet_id` (default `SHEET_ID`), new action `POST admin/sheets/spreadsheet` (id or full address). The push path now checks the configured id. Changing it drops the pending ack and saved choices; the baseline stays. Pausing/resuming no longer resets the id.
- **When:** `POST admin/sheets/pull` ("סנכרון עכשיו"), `POST admin/sheets/test` (reads only, says how many sites/columns, writes nothing), `POST/DELETE admin/sheets/service-account`. Automatic: the minute timer `maintenance/scheduled-articles` starts `scheduledSheetsPull()` in `waitUntil`; it runs only when the sync is on, a key is stored and 5 minutes passed since the last start. Lock `sheets_pull_lock` (5 minutes) prevents two runs. State in `sheets_pull_state`.
- **Google errors in Hebrew:** API not enabled (403 SERVICE_DISABLED / "has not been used"), not shared (403), shared as viewer only (403 on a write), sheet not found (404), tab missing, 401 (one retry with a new token, then "paste the key again"), 429, 5xx, no connection, token errors (bad signature / deleted key, account not found or disabled, server clock).
- **Screen (`app/catalog-sheets.tsx`, `.css`):** new card "חיבור ישיר לגוגל (מומלץ)": steps, key box (masked, cleared after save, never shown back), service account e-mail with copy and "שתפו את השיטס עם הכתובת הזו כעורך", replace/delete key, sheet id, "בדיקת חיבור", "סנכרון עכשיו", last run line. Every button shows busy and done/failed. Step 1 points to the direct card; the Apps Script instructions are titled "חלופה". "פתיחת השיטס" opens the configured sheet. Checked locally desktop 1440 and phone 375 (no horizontal scroll) with a throwaway key, removed afterwards; "בדיקת חיבור"/"סנכרון עכשיו" not clicked there (they would call Google).
- **Files:** new `lib/sheets-google.ts`, `lib/sheets-account.ts`, `tests/sheets-service-account.mjs`; changed `lib/sheets-sync.ts`, `app/api/[...path]/route.ts`, `app/catalog-sheets.tsx`, `app/catalog-sheets.css`. `.claude/launch.json` got a local `qa-local` entry (local only).
- **Tests:** new `tests/sheets-service-account.mjs` (161 checks, fake Google through Miniflare `outboundService`, RSA key made at test time, JWT verified with its public key). Passing: sheets-service-account, sheets-plan, sheets-arrange, sheets-script-roundtrip, sheets-columns, sheets-two-way, sheets-sync-integration, sheets-merge-properties, resend-email, gemini-secrets. `tsc` clean, build passes.
- **Not checked:** real Google (never called). Assumptions to confirm on the test copy: the FORMULA read returns a text cell like "=1+1" as the same text (so it is not taken for a formula); USER_ENTERED turns "2026-11-30" into a date like setValue does. On Staging: whether `waitUntil` keeps running after the 15-second timer request ends (if not, the automatic pull may be cut on a large sheet; the manual button is not affected).
- **Next:** owner pastes the JSON key in the admin on Staging, sets the sheet id to the test copy, "בדיקת חיבור", "סנכרון עכשיו", then turns the sync on. Not committed, not pushed, not deployed.
- **Review fix (after the agent, 16.11.2026):** changing the sheet id in the admin now also drops the old sheet's baseline, snapshot, sync state and receipt (`lib/sheets-sync.ts`), so a new sheet starts like a first connection and every site/sheet difference waits for the owner's choice. Check added to `tests/sheets-service-account.mjs` (170 checks). Passing after a fresh build: sheets-service-account, sheets-plan, sheets-arrange, sheets-columns, sheets-script-roundtrip, sheets-two-way, sheets-sync-integration, sheets-merge-properties, resend-email, gemini-secrets, admin-lobby-panels; tsc clean.
- **Staging needs no gateway opening for this:** the site calls Google outbound; the every-minute timer in `scripts/railway-staging-start.mjs` already calls `maintenance/scheduled-articles`, which now starts the five-minute pull.

## 20.11.2026 (44): No N+1 queries (branch `claude/n-plus-one-20261120`, from the deployed 0f9c50a)
- **Why:** the owner's scheduled instruction for 05:00: "When fetching relations or linked data, you must absolutely avoid the N+1 problem. Never use iterative requests. Use JOINs or Eager Loading." Every place that read D1 inside a loop (one query per row) now reads each relation once, with a JOIN or `IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?))` (one JSON parameter, so no limit on the number of ids). Behaviour is meant to stay exactly the same.
- **Helpers (`lib/server.ts`):** `allIn(table,column,values,where?,...args)` and `mapIn(...)` (one query, a Map by that column; table and column names are checked against `^[a-z_]+$`), `websitesById(ids,allowInactive?)` (same checks and pricing as `website()`, in the same order as the ids), `settingsByKey(keys,fallbacks)`.
- **Places fixed:**
  - `app/api/[...path]/route.ts`: bundle save, adding a bundle to the cart, PR cart, embedded images in an article, files attached to a brief, bundles in the cart, supplier-stock retry, integrations screen settings.
  - `lib/order-create.ts`: the order's sites and their suppliers.
  - `lib/checks.ts`: image files of an article check. `lib/knowledge-server.ts`: guide images.
  - `lib/marketing-consent.ts`: users by e-mail in one query. `lib/monthly.ts`: month close reads all the month's charges once.
  - `lib/pricing.ts`: sites and earlier overrides of a pricing change. `lib/account-management.ts`: sites of an admin quote.
  - `lib/scheduled-dispatch.ts`, `lib/scheduled-approval.ts`: orders of the due articles (the article after the claim is built from the claimed row: same version guard, so it is the same row).
  - `lib/admin-updates.ts`: the updates feed read in one query. `lib/payment-reports.ts` + `lib/payment-reference.ts` (`referenceUsesMany`): duplicate bank references for all pending reports in two queries.
  - `lib/admin-control-center.ts`: the queue counts of the control centre in one `UNION ALL` query (two in total, was about 20).
  - `lib/customer-sellers.ts`: monthly seller statements (`publicationsMany`: profiles and articles in two queries; seller e-mail joined into the pairs query).
  - `lib/supplier-stock.ts` (`siteStocks(ids)`, `siteStock` now calls it) and `lib/supplier-orders.ts`: a supplier's stock packages read 7 queries in total instead of 7 per site. The latest 50 releases per site use `ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY ...)`.
  - `lib/admin-content.ts`: stock packages of a supplier's sites in one query.
- **Left as is, on purpose:** writes inside loops (each needs its own guard and result, for example `INSERT OR IGNORE` with `meta.changes`), loops whose list itself comes from one query, and sending mails one by one.
- **Checks:** `pnpm exec tsc --noEmit` OK, `pnpm build` OK, the 3 Railway tests OK. Full run: the same 60 older failures, no new one. A first run found that D1 refuses a `UNION ALL` of more than a few terms ("too many terms in compound SELECT"), so the control-centre counts use the existing `adminUnionCtes` (CTEs of 3).
- **CLAUDE.md:** the owner approved adding the N+1 rule (20.11.2026); it is under "Rules".
- **Next:** nothing pushed or deployed. Push and Staging only after the owner approves.

## 20.11.2026 (45): N+1, a second, deeper round (same branch; owner: "תבצע סבב נוסף ומעמיק יותר", push not approved)
- **How:** a scan with the TypeScript checker (scratchpad `scan-typed.cjs`): every function that reaches D1 directly or through other functions, then every call to one inside a loop. Also browser code that calls the API inside a loop.
- **Fixed:**
  - `lib/supplier-orders.ts` + `lib/admin-suppliers.ts`: a supplier's billing rows were loaded page by page, and every page reloaded all the settlement data (up to 49 full reloads). Now one call (`supplierSettlements(q,true)`), same 49×20 cap as before.
  - `app/api/[...path]/route.ts` `validBrief`: in a bundle or PR order (one brief per site) the customer, its used sites and the attached files were read again for every site. Now once per request (`BriefCache`); `lib/agency.ts` split `usedSiteHit` into `usedSitesOf` + `usedSiteHitIn`.
  - `lib/mail.ts`: a mail's attachment files read in one query; `articleMail` takes the order and admin list from the caller (`lib/scheduled-approval.ts` reads the admin list once) and reads the customer only for customer mails.
  - `lib/marketing-consent.ts`: removing pasted addresses reads all consent rows in one query and writes all changes in one batch (`marketingStatements`; `setMarketing` unchanged for one user).
  - Interview done (`route.ts`): the activity rows of the moved articles written in one batch.
  - Package screen "תזכורת לספק" (`app/supplier-v2.tsx`): one request to the new `POST /api/admin/supplier-remind` (admin only, same checks as the single reminder, all checked before anything is sent) instead of one request per article.
- **Left as is, on purpose (each is one business action per item with its own guard or an outside effect):** sending each mail (`queueMail`/`sendQueuedMail`, one job per recipient), sending each article to its supplier (`dispatchArticle`), closing each customer's month (`closeCustomerMonth`, atomic per customer, once a month), the supplier month mails, catalog saves in chunks of 100, D1 batches in chunks of 20.
- **Tests:** new `tests/n-plus-one-batch.mjs` (bulk reminder and bulk removal, fictional data) passes. `tests/supplier-mail.mjs` mock now exports `mapIn`. tsc, build, 3 Railway tests OK; full run: the same 60 older failures, no new one.
- **Next:** nothing pushed or deployed; waiting for the owner.

## Support ticket windows on a phone (20.11.2026)

- **Owner:** "on a phone it opens annoyingly and needs zooming out, and the screen isn't pretty", then "use nicer fonts".
- **Causes:**
  - The header icon (sparkles) sat on the close button.
  - Focus landed on the close button on open and drew a ring; on an open ticket the keyboard could jump into the field.
  - Fields under 16px (14px and 15px) make the iPhone zoom in.
  - The font was Arial, the site default.
- **Changed (only the conversation window and the "פתיחת פנייה" window):**
  - `app/ticket-conversation.css`: a frame like the cancel request window (white header with a blue badge, close button on the left, only the body scrolls). On a phone (under 640px) the window opens as a sheet from the bottom at full width; fields at 16px; Heebo font; quieter text instead of the site-wide 16px bold dialog rule. The closed-ticket box and the edit row were redone.
  - `app/dialog-badge.tsx`: support windows show a headset icon instead of sparkles.
  - `app/workspace.tsx`: support windows take focus themselves on open (`onOpenAutoFocus`), so no ring and no keyboard jump.
  - `app/ticket-conversation.tsx`: the edit row reads "עריכת פרטי הפנייה" with a quiet hint "נושא, פירוט, מספר הזמנה".
  - `app/globals.css`: the "הפניות שלי" page uses Heebo.
- **Checked locally** at 390px and on desktop: closed ticket, open ticket with a long thread, new ticket. No horizontal overflow, fields at 16px, focus on the window.
- **Not deployed.** It goes up together with the accessibility work and the separate suppliers (`0a43dae`).
- **Owner approved the design (20.11)** with two notes: make sure it is centered on a phone, and the bottom button blue in the site's style, not turquoise.
  - The send button of the ticket form (`.support-form-actions` submit in `app/globals.css`) is now the site blue `#1673e6` with a darker hover, instead of turquoise `#69f4dc`.

## 20.11.2026 (46): Accessibility to WCAG 2.2 AA, new accessibility statement and terms wording (branch `claude/accessibility-terms-c15be3`, from 209f04f)
- **Owner:** the old statement said openly that not everything works ("נגיש באופן חלקי", 18 open problems); fix everything to the strictest standard, and have GPT (ChatGPT in the owner's Chrome, strongest model) review and rewrite the statement and the terms in professional Hebrew. GPT writes and reviews text only; code is ours.
- **How it was checked (local, fictional data, own QA copy on ports 5190/8797 with a copy of local-state):** axe-core 4.11 with WCAG 2.0/2.1/2.2 A+AA + best practice on 33 screens × desktop/phone; pixel-measured contrast for text on gradients; keyboard walk (skip link, visible focus by screenshot diff, focus not hidden); 12 dialogs (focus in, trap, Escape, focus return); reflow 320px; text spacing. Scripts in the session scratchpad (`a11y-tools/`), not in the repo.
- **Fixed in code:**
  - Focus returns to the opener after closing any dialog/sheet/alert (`components/ui/focus-return.ts`, used by dialog/sheet/alert-dialog). Moving between screens moves focus to the new screen's h1 (`app/workspace.tsx`).
  - Skip link "דילוג לתוכן הראשי" on every system and knowledge page (`#main-content`, `#knowledge-content`); styles in `app/accessibility-menu.css`.
  - Own page title for every customer screen (`customerPageTitle` in `lib/seo.ts`, also server-side in `app/[...page]/page.tsx`).
  - Contrast: button gradients darkened (orange #ffa04a→#f26a1b is now #d14509→#b93d08, same for green, teal, blue, red); amber buttons keep amber with dark text; faint greys and the brand blue #1673e6→#1467cf; cyan gradient text; home/PR colours. All text measured ≥4.5:1 (large ≥3:1).
  - Structure: Hebrew 404 page (`app/not-found.tsx`), no nested main in the editor, signed-out page has an h1, one h1 in support, checkout h2, unique table names on legal pages, asides not nested, floating buttons in a labelled region.
  - Names: orders list rows read the whole row, seller tabs on phone, copy buttons ("העתקה: …"), account menu (visible text in the name), phone menu ("תפריט האתר"), toaster ("התראות"), ticket without subject.
  - Forms: Hebrew validation messages site-wide (`app/form-messages.tsx` in the layout), "(חובה)" instead of an unexplained "*", aria-required, autocomplete in account settings, image description (alt) field for images inside an article (editor, inline mode).
  - Keyboard: DR/UR tips close with Escape, accessibility panel moves focus inside, PR site row scrollable by keyboard, a11y button no longer covers WhatsApp in the editor, scroll-padding so focus is not hidden under the sticky header.
  - Home: accessibility statement + privacy links in the footer and in the a11y panel; rotating hero phrases stop with "עצירת אנימציות".
  - Order PDF: language he-IL and title shown.
- **Texts (GPT-6.2 Astra, Max effort, in the owner's ChatGPT; business details replaced by placeholders before sending):**
  - Accessibility statement rewritten from the verified facts only (`design/legal/drafts/accessibility.md`, version 2.0, 20.11.2026): scope, IS 5568 AA + WCAG 2.2 AA, how it was checked, the adjustments, coordinator contact (no login needed), demand under reg. 35A(d)(1), accessible documents within 7 business days, third parties with phone/e-mail alternatives. No "partial", "in progress" or "not checked". Page summary in `lib/legal-docs.ts` no longer says "מה עדיין מוגבל".
  - Terms rewritten in formal legal Hebrew (`design/legal/drafts/terms.md`, version 1.1, 20.11.2026), same 31 sections and 134 sub-sections, business decisions unchanged. Added only statutory mentions: consumer cancellation per 14ג/14ג1 (4 months for disabled, senior, new immigrant), refund in 14 days, no cancellation fee from consumers (as in the cancellation policy), 30א marketing rules, privacy access/correction, accessibility coordinator in 28.3.
  - "ביטול עסקה" link added to the site footer and the home footer (Consumer Protection Law 14ט: a dedicated cancellation link on the home page), pointing to the cancellation policy; terms 29.4 says so.
- **GPT's legal findings that need the owner (not changed by us):** (1) prices to consumers must be shown including VAT (17א/17ד), the catalog shows "לפני מע״מ"; (2) if "Mega Links" is a trade name, the registered owner's name should appear next to the VAT id; (3) article label: "פרסומת" is safer than "תוכן שיווקי" (7(ג)(2)); (4) privacy notices at the points where login, phone and bank proofs are collected; (5) "אתר שווה ערך" is not defined. Coordinator name: none given, the statement says "רכז הנגישות של מגה לינקס".
- **Checks:** axe 0 violations on all 34 screens × desktop/phone (was ~900 failing elements); pixel contrast clean apart from verified false positives (disabled button, logo text, gradient headings ≥3:1 checked by hand); 12 dialogs pass focus-in/trap/Escape/return; keyboard walks with visible, unhidden focus; reflow 320px and text spacing without loss (only the support ticket preview is clamped, full text opens in the ticket). tsc, build, the 3 Railway tests, seo (67), knowledge (55) and 11 more UI tests pass. catalog-workspace-actions and article-customer-assignment-ui fail exactly as on HEAD (checked on a clean copy).

## 22.11.2026: four iPhone fixes from the owner's screenshots, merged into `claude/iphone-fixes-20261122` (from a098243)

The owner sent four iPhone screenshots and asked for each fix in its own session, then approved all four together ("כן תעלה"). Each session committed on its own branch; the coordinating session merged them here.

### Agency customers table on a phone (branch `claude/agency-customers-table-mobile-5fdf67`)

- **Bug (owner screenshot, iPhone):** admin → customer → "הלקוחות שלו" → "לקוחות הסוכנות": every column broke one letter per line. Cause: the admin table rule `.admin-design-v172 .admin-editorial-surface :is(td,…)` (20px 18px padding, `overflow-wrap:anywhere`) on seven columns at 390px.
- **Fix:** `app/customer-account-admin.tsx` table gets class `agency-customers-table` and `data-label` on each cell; `app/globals.css` (end of file, `@media(max-width:767px)` only): each customer is a white rounded card, name on top, then "label: value" lines. Doubled class for specificity over the admin rule. Desktop untouched.
- **Checked locally** (own QA server on 5181/8788, own state folder in the scratchpad, fictional agency customers on `qa-customer`): 390px readable, long domains wrap, no sideways scroll; desktop the same.
- **Before upload:** nothing temporary in the code.

### Customer file, "פרטי החשבון" card on the phone (branch `claude/customer-details-form-mobile-3b139d`)
- **Problem (owner's iPhone):** the e-mail sat under the save button with no label; the fields (name, company, phone) had no visible box.
- **Cause:** `.cf .panel input` (`app/customer-file-v2.css`) removes the border and expects a grey fill, but the shared admin rule `.admin-design-v172 ... input` forces a white background. White field on a white card = invisible. The shared rule was not touched.
- **Fix, this screen only:** a stronger `.cf.cf.cf.cf .panel .field` rule keeps the grey fill and adds a thin frame; the e-mail is now a read-only "מייל" field at the top of the card (still not editable, as before). Desktop checked at 1280px.
- **Files:** `app/customer-account-admin.tsx`, `app/customer-file-v2.css`.
- **Status:** checked locally with fictional data.

### Site edit form fields (branch `claude/site-edit-fields-mobile-fcda11`)
- **Problem (owner's iPhone):** "חיבור WordPress", "הנחיות ומגבלות פרסום", "הערות לאתר", "הערות ללקוח", "כתבות לדוגמה" showed only a label and a white gap. Same cause as the customer file: the shared admin rule (`app/admin-editorial.css:36`) paints fields white on a white card. Invisible on desktop too.
- **Fix, this form only:** two lines in `app/catalog-v2-editors.css` give the textareas and the select the grey fill the other fields have. The shared rule was not touched.

### Customer orders tab buttons (branch `claude/orders-tab-buttons-mobile-f82857`)
- **Problem (owner: "הכפתורים שם קצת מכוסים, צריך שיהיה מרווח נורמלי"):** the selected filter chip's border was cut at the card edge and the export and new-order buttons sat tight against the chips.
- **Fix:** `app/customer-file-v2.css` only, phone widths: chips inside the card padding, normal gaps. Wide desktop unchanged.

### Deployed
- **Staging (owner: "כן תעלה", 22.11):** merge `b99ccb7`, deployment `cb722626`, SUCCESS. Build passed on the merged branch; each fix was checked locally by its own session at 390px and desktop. Not checked on Staging itself. Staging now follows `claude/iphone-fixes-20261122` (pinned to `b99ccb7`). The e-mail reply-to commit `34c9f71` on `claude/reply-to-20261122` is not part of it.

### Open
- The shared admin field rule (`.admin-design-v172 … input/textarea/select` white background) can hide fields on any screen whose card is white. Fixed screen by screen here; a shared fix needs the owner's OK.

## החזרה לעריכה של כתבה מאושרת (22.11.2026, branch `claude/reopen-approved-article-a85857`)
- **מה הוחלט (הבעלים):** מנהל יכול להחזיר ללקוח לעריכה כתבה שאושרה ועוד לא פורסמה. כשהיא כבר אצל הספק, הקישור שלו לא מבוטל אלא מציג "נא להמתין עם פרסום הכתבה", המלאי נשאר תפוס, והספק מקבל מייל באותו שרשור. אחרי אישור חוזר הספק מקבל "הנחיות מעודכנות לפרסום" על אותו קישור (תוקף מוארך ב-30 יום). אם הספק כבר פרסם את הגרסה הקודמת, המנהל מטפל בזה מולו בנפרד.
- **זמין:** אושרה וממתינה לתאריך, לא הגיעה לספק, אצל הספק. **לא זמין:** פורסמה, הדגמה, בוטלה, בקשת ביטול.
- **קבצים:** `lib/article-reopen.ts` (חדש: `reopenApprovedArticle`, `supplierFollowUp`), `lib/publication.ts` (`resumeHeldArticle` בתוך `dispatchArticle`), `lib/mail.ts` (`supplierThreadId`, כותרות שרשור ב-`sendQueuedMail` וב-`resendMessage`), `lib/supplier-mail-content.ts` (`supplierHoldEmailHtml` חדשה, פרמטר `update` ב-`supplierArticleEmailHtml`), `app/api/[...path]/route.ts` (`POST articles/:id/reopen`, `waiting_fixes` בעמוד הספק), `app/supplier-report-page.tsx/.css` (`SupplierHold`), `app/admin-article-decision.tsx` (`ReopenAction`), `tests/article-reopen.mjs`.
- **שימו לב:** שדה עם המילה supplier מסונן בתשובות ללא מנהל (`customer-privacy.ts`), ולכן בעמוד הספק השדה נקרא `waiting_fixes`.
- **כתבת עורך (editorial):** חוזרת ל-`review` ולא ל-`revisions`, כדי שלא תיכנס לתור הכותב.
- **שרשור (הבעלים):** שרשור אחד לכל כתבה, לעולם לא לפי ספק או משלוח. נושא המייל הראשון ייחודי לכתבה: "כתבה לפרסום באתר <אתר> <מונה> · <כותרת>". המזהה `<supplier-article-<id>@<דומיין השולח>>` נשמר במשימת המייל הראשון (`thread_id`, `thread_subject`). המתנה, עדכון ותזכורת נשלחים עם "Re:" ו-In-Reply-To. כתבה שנשלחה לפני השינוי (אין מזהה שמור) מקבלת מייל חדש בלי "Re:", עם נושא ייחודי ("נא להמתין עם פרסום הכתבה · ..." / "הנחיות מעודכנות · ..."). בדיקה: `tests/supplier-thread.mjs`.
- **לא נבדק:** ש-Resend מכבד `Message-ID` מותאם. לבדוק ב-Staging עם תיבת הבדיקה.
- **בדיקות:** `tests/article-reopen.mjs` עוברת (ב-Windows דרך עותק harness עם `pathToFileURL`), וגם `publication-scheduling`, `supplier-mail`, `resend-email`. בדיקה מקומית מלאה עם נתוני דמה: החזרה, עמוד ספק בהמתנה, שליחה מחדש, אישור חוזר, הארכת תוקף. המיילים נחסמו מקומית (אין חיבור דיוור).
- **לפני העלאה:** אין שאריות זמניות ב-repo. לא בוצע commit.

## בקשת תיקונים / דחייה מהספק (22.11.2026, branch `claude/supplier-fix-or-reject-7c9a90`)
- **מה הוחלט (הבעלים):** הספק יכול לבקש תיקונים או לבקש לדחות כתבה, עם טקסט חובה. הבקשה מגיעה רק למנהל: לא משנה הזמנה, לא מבטלת, לא מזכה ולא מודיעה ללקוח. בקשה פתוחה אחת בכל פעם.
- **מייל הכתבה:** הכפתור הכחול נשאר. מתחתיו שורה אחת "יש בעיה בכתבה? בקשת תיקונים או דחיית הכתבה", לאותו קישור אישי עם `#issue` (העמוד נפתח על בקשת תיקונים). רק לקישור אישי (`/supplier/<token>`), לא לפורטל ההדגמה.
- **עמוד הספק:** שלוש אפשרויות: הוספת קישור (כמו היום), בקשת תיקונים, דחיית הכתבה. אחרי שליחה: "הבקשה נשלחה · ממתין לתשובת המנהל", והדבקת קישור חסומה גם בשרת. בזמן שהכתבה אצל הלקוח (`waiting_fixes` של reopen) כל האפשרויות מוסתרות. בבקשת ביטול של לקוח מוצג רק הטופס הישן.
- **מנהל:** מרכז העבודה: "הספק ביקש לדחות כתבה" בדחוף, "הספק ביקש תיקונים" בתוכן. מסך הכתבה (שלב "אצל הספק"): תיבה עם הטקסט. תיקונים: "החזרה לעריכה של הלקוח" פותחת את `ReopenAction` עם דברי הספק, ו"הספק יפרסם כמו שהיא" מבקשת הערה שנשלחת לספק. דחייה: "אישור ביטול וזיכוי" פותח את חלון הביטול הקיים, ו"סירוב" מבקש הערה שנשלחת לספק. גם מייל למנהלים כשנפתחת בקשה.
- **סגירה:** החזרה לעריכה סוגרת את הבקשה (`closeIssueOnReopen` בנתיב `reopen`). ביטול, בקשת ביטול או גרסה מעודכנת (`supplier_updated_at`) הופכים בקשה ישנה ללא פעילה (`stale`), בלי לגעת בתהליך הביטול.
- **פרטיות:** יומן הפעולות נרשם על `supplier-issue:<article>` ולא על הכתבה, כי הלקוח רואה את יומן הכתבה. בעמוד הספק השדה נקרא `issue` ובמנהל `issue_request` (שדות עם supplier מסוננים).
- **קבצים:** חדשים: `lib/supplier-article-request.ts`, `lib/supplier-issue-mail.ts`, `app/supplier-issue.tsx/.css`, `app/admin-supplier-issue.tsx/.css`, `tests/supplier-article-request.mjs`. שונו: `app/api/[...path]/route.ts`, `app/supplier-report-page.tsx`, `app/admin-article-decision.tsx` (תיבה בשלב הספק, האזנה ב-`ReopenAction`), `lib/admin-updates.ts`, `app/admin-update-feed.tsx`, `lib/supplier-mail-content.ts` (שורה אחת), `lib/mail.ts` (שורת טקסט ומייל תשובה `supplier_followup:'answer'`).
- **בדיקות:** `tests/supplier-article-request.mjs` ו-`tests/article-reopen.mjs` עוברות (ב-Windows רק עם תיקון זמני של `pathToFileURL` ב-harness, שלא נשמר). עוברות גם `supplier-thread`, `admin-lobby-panels`, `payment-reports`, `admin-glass`, `monthly-order-lifecycle`. בדיקה מקומית ב-390 עם נתוני דמה: תיקונים, דחייה, המתנה, החזרה ללקוח, סירוב, מרכז העבודה. המיילים נחסמו מקומית (אין חיבור דיוור, כתובות example.test).
- **במסד המקומי בלבד:** שלוש רשומות יומן ישנות שנרשמו על הכתבה עצמה בניסיון הראשון (לפני תיקון הפרטיות). אין שאריות זמניות ב-repo.
- **לא נבדק:** השלמת ביטול וזיכוי מלאה מתוך בקשת דחייה (רק פתיחת החלון), ומייל אמיתי ב-Staging.

## חלון "מחיר אישי" ללקוח (22.11.2026, ענף claude/personal-price-dialog-afcd59)
- **מה הוחלט:** הבעלים אישר הדמיה. שדה המחיר בראש החלון, ארבעת המחירים לעיון בכרטיס אחד, הערת מע״מ קצרה, שמירה וביטול בסגנון "עריכה מהירה".

- **זמני:** אין.
## 22.11.2026: fix-request flow for the customer (branch `claude/fix-request-flow-a67639`, from `0d0b152`)
- **Owner asked:** when the admin returns an article for a fix, the customer gets a clear email ("המאמר שלך לאתר X דורש תיקון"), a popup in "העדכונים שלך" in the approved popup style, and the admin's note at the top of the editor. Later also: "פרטי הביטול" in the same new style. Mockups approved ("מאשר את כל השאר").
- **Email (`lib/fix-request-mail.ts` new, `lib/mail.ts`):** `articleMail(a,'revisions',origin,note)` keeps its name and inputs and now goes to `revisionMail`: subject `fixRequestSubject(site)`, its own HTML (`fixRequestEmailHtml`): site + address, article title, order, a box "מה צריך לתקן", button to `/article/<id>`, and a service footer (why the mail came, personal area link, reply for questions; no unsubscribe, it is a service mail). Plain text `fixRequestText` in the same order. Published/submitted mails unchanged.
- **The note is kept on the article:** `revisionMail` writes `revision_note` and `revision_requested_at` into `articles.data` (no version change), so every path that returns an article (also the sibling "return an approved article" on `claude/reopen-approved-article-a85857`) fills the popup and the editor. Added to the customer whitelist in `lib/customer-privacy.ts`.
- **Updates feed (`lib/client-updates.ts`, `app/client-updates.tsx`):** the note comes from the article; older returns still read it from the email (old subject or the new one). Popup "נדרש תיקון במאמר" (`app/customer-revision-dialog.tsx` new) and "פרטי הביטול" open with `sky:true`; their look is in `app/customer-updates-sky.css` (new, loaded in `app/layout.tsx`): body scrolls, actions in a bottom strip, so the admin's note is never behind the close button. Icon for the new popup: `kind:'article-revision'` in `app/dialog-badge.tsx`.
- **Editor (`app/article-fix-note.tsx/.css` new, one line in `app/article-studio.tsx`):** customer only, status `revisions` with a note: a yellow card under the header, open at first, folds to one line.
- **Checked locally (fictional data, 390px and 1280px):** both popups, editor open/closed, the email HTML taken from the local job (mail connection off, nothing sent). `tests/fix-request-mail.mjs` new, passes; tsc and build pass. `tests/client-updates.mjs` fails on an old cart assertion about `app/workspace.tsx`, same on `0d0b152` (not related).
- **Not pushed, not deployed.**
- **Open:** a writer's article (editorial) that the sibling returns goes to `review`, not `revisions`, so it shows no fix note in the popup or editor. The email still goes out.
## עורך הכתבה בטלפון: העתקת טקסט וכותרת שלא עולה (22.11.2026, ענף `claude/editor-title-mobile-275888`, לא ב-commit)
- **באג 1 (העתקה בטלפון):** גוף הכתבה היה תיבת גלילה פנימית של 340px עם `overscroll-behavior:contain`, והסרגל הדביק (106px) כיסה את תחילתה. כשהדף נגלל מתחת לסרגל, הכותרת בראש הכתבה נשארה מוסתרת, וגרירה על הטקסט לא הזיזה את הדף.
  - **התיקון:** מתחת ל-700px הגוף גדל עם הטקסט והדף גולל אותו. הסרגל נשאר דביק. נוסף `scroll-padding-top` כשהעורך בפוקוס. רק CSS, בסוף `app/studio-v6.css`. במחשב לא השתנה.
- **באג 2 (הכותרת לא עולה):** בייבוא Word הכותרת נלקחה רק מסגנון "Heading 1" או "Title". כותב שסימן את הכותרת כ-Heading 2 או כשורה מודגשת, קיבל שדה כותרת ריק, והכותרת נשארה בגוף.
  - **התיקון:** `leadingTitle` ב-`app/article-studio.tsx`. אם אין H1, לוקחים את הבלוק הראשון כשהוא כותרת (H1–H6) או שורה מודגשת כולה, קצרה, בלי נקודה בסוף. הכותרת יורדת מהגוף כמו קודם.
  - **כתבות שכבר יובאו (לפי משוב הבעלים):** כששדה הכותרת ריק בפתיחת העורך:
    - **כותרת H1 או H2 בראש הגוף:** עוברת לשדה לבד, פעם אחת לכל כתבה, ונשמרת כמו עריכה רגילה. אין ביטול בחזרה, כי היסטוריית העורך מתאפסת כשהתוכן מוחלף מבחוץ (`rich-editor.tsx`, שלא שיניתי).
    - **מקרה לא בטוח (H3–H6 או שורה מודגשת):** מופיע הכפתור "העברת הכותרת מהמאמר". העיצוב ב-`app/studio-v4.css` (`.sv-title-suggest`).
    - **כשמקלידים כותרת:** הכפתור נעלם.
- **נבדק מקומית ב-390px:** לפני ואחרי, והכפתור עובד. בנייה עברה. `studio-ui` ו-`studio-actions` נכשלות גם בלי השינוי (`refund-dialog` מול fixture של `workspace`).
- **לפני העלאה:** אין שאריות זמניות. בטיוטה המקומית 2192088b נשמרו שינויי בדיקה בנתונים בדויים בלבד.
