## 22.11.2026: supplier article e-mail, Word RTL, images attached, credits line

Owner: "הגיע אבל לא מיושר לעברית. והתמונה עצמה לא צורפה למייל ... שורה מודגשת של קרדיטים לתמונות". Branch `claude/supplier-mail-files-03afb3`.
- **Word file right-to-left** (`lib/rich-docx.ts`): Hebrew is written the way Word itself writes it, so the iPhone preview and Google Docs follow it: `<w:bidi/>` (not `w:val="1"`) on every Hebrew paragraph, `<w:rtl/>` + `w:lang w:bidi="he-IL"` on Hebrew runs, no `w:jc` for the natural right alignment (the old `w:jc="start"` is a strict-only value the iPhone preview read as left), RTL table (`w:bidiVisual`), RTL document default (`pPrDefault` bidi, he-IL language), run and table properties in schema order. Line spacing kept (after 160, 1.08). Checked by converting with Word on this PC: page right-aligned, table columns from the right. Not checked on a real iPhone (needs Staging).
- **Images as separate files** (`lib/mail.ts`): cause: only `image_id`/`secondary_image_id` were attached, never the pictures inside the text (the editor's images), and above 15MB nothing at all was attached. Now `articleImages()` lists main, secondary and every `/api/files/<id>` inside the text, once each (max 20), named `image-1.jpg`, `image-2.jpg`...; files are attached in order (Word first) while they fit 15MB (Resend) / 6MB (webhook); a file that does not fit keeps its download link and the e-mail says "לא נכנסו למייל בגלל הגודל (התמונות נמצאות גם בקובץ ה-Word)". Download-link index limit raised from 10 to 30 files.
- **Credits** (`lib/supplier-mail-content.ts`): one bold "קרדיטים לתמונות:" row with every credit (main + its URL, secondary, each inline `data-credit`), duplicates once; it replaces the two separate rows "קרדיט לתמונה הראשית/נוספת". Same in the plain-text version.
- **Tests:** `tests/rich-docx.mjs` asserts RTL; `tests/resend-email.mjs` asserts partial attachment, inline images attached, credits line, thread header kept (45 checks). `supplier-mail`, `fix-request-mail`, `writing-workflow` pass. `article-reopen` could not run here (Windows path loader), it stubs `./mail` anyway.
- **Images:** shared notes folder `supplier-mail-files/` (e-mail before/after at 390px, Word page before/after).
- **Same thread (owner: "התיקון לא הופיע באותו שרשור")** (`lib/mail.ts`): on Staging the test inbox mode put "[בדיקה · אל: x] " before "Re:", so the follow-up subject did not match the first e-mail and Gmail opened a new conversation. Now the test mark goes after a leading "Re: " ("Re: [בדיקה · אל: x] <same subject>"). Also: an article pulled back with the old status route (first job renamed ":recalled:") and approved again used to send a new first e-mail with the same fixed Message-ID (new thread, and Gmail may drop a repeated Message-ID); now it answers the stored thread ("Re:", In-Reply-To/References, no new Message-ID). Still one thread per article. Test: `tests/supplier-thread.mjs` (test mode and real mode).
- **Open:** Resend's docs do not say whether a custom `Message-ID` header is kept (they document In-Reply-To/References via `headers`, and the send response returns only Resend's own id). Check once on Staging: Gmail > "Show original" on the first supplier e-mail should show `Message-ID: <supplier-article-<id>@<sender domain>>`. If Resend replaced it, threading needs another approach.
## 22.11.2026: supplier link check ("האתר לא השיב") and the supplier link page

- **Cause (owner's report):** on Staging the check timed out after 8 s (plain server fetch, Range header, no browser User-Agent; the site is behind Cloudflare). It never reached the content checks, so it did not detect the other article.
- **Fix (`lib/checks.ts`):** one fetch of the page instead of two, browser headers (User-Agent, Accept, Accept-Language he), no Range, 15 s per request, one retry on timeout or network error. Safety unchanged (`externalUrl`, 3 redirects, expected domain).
- **Messages, one per cause:** site did not answer in time (technical, try again later); page not found (404/410); site refused the check (401/403/429); server error (5xx); other HTTP; another domain or a redirect to one; bad redirect; links missing (list); "נראה שהקישור מוביל לכתבה אחרת" with the page title found and the article title. Topic is now checked before the links. Results carry `reason`.
- **Supplier page (`app/supplier-report-page.tsx` + css):** card shows "נושא הכתבה" with the full title and "האתר שבו מפרסמים" with name, domain and a link to the site (new `website_url` in `GET /api/supplier/<token>`).
- **Test:** `tests/supplier-link-check.mjs` (mocked fetch and a small HTMLRewriter stand-in). Local run: a different real pcmag article returns the "another article" message in 0.4 s.
- **Not built (proposal for the owner):** a site that can never be checked automatically → accept the link as "ממתין לבדיקה ידנית" for the admin to confirm.
- **Before upload:** nothing temporary in the repo.
## סיבת "הכתבה לא הגיעה לספק" (22.11.2026, אושר: "כן")
- **מה:** במסך הכתבה של המנהל, בתיבה "הכתבה לא הגיעה לספק", מוצגת עכשיו הסיבה האמיתית שנשמרה בשליחה (`jobs` `publication:<id>`, `data.error`, רק כשהסטטוס `blocked` או `failed`), במקום הניחוש הכללי. מתחתיה שורה מה לעשות. בסיבות של האתר (הסכם לא מאומת, אין מייל, מייל לא תקין) יש קישור "לעריכת האתר", שפותח את עריכת האתר אצל הספק שלו. שני הכפתורים נשארו. בלי סיבה שמורה, הטקסט הישן נשאר.
- **שליחה חוזרת:** אם חזרה חסומה, הסיבה מוצגת מיד בתיבה ובהודעה אדומה, בלי "עודכן" ירוק. בהצלחה: "הכתבה נשלחה לספק".
- **שרת:** `adminArticleExtras` ב-`lib/publication.ts`, שאילתה אחת (שם הלקוח + סטטוס וסיבת המסירה, LEFT JOIN), רק למנהל. מחליפה את שאילתת שם הלקוח, אז אין שאילתה נוספת.
- **קבצים:** `lib/delivery-reason.ts` (חדש), `lib/publication.ts`, `app/api/[...path]/route.ts`, `app/admin-article-decision.tsx`, `app/admin-article-review.css`, `tests/delivery-reason.mjs` (חדש).
- **בדיקות:** `tests/delivery-reason.mjs`, `article-reopen`, `supplier-article-request` עוברות. `admin-content` נכשלת גם לפני השינוי (טיוטה מוסתרת, לא קשור).
- **לא גמור:** "אין מייל ספק" כמעט לא יקרה, כי יש כתובת ספק ברירת מחדל. נבדק רק דרך סיבה שמורה.

- **Supplier e-mail and Word** (`claude/supplier-mail-files-03afb3`, `bded278` + `c519b27`): Word file right-to-left; images attached as separate files (what doesn't fit is named and stays in the Word); bold credits line; follow-up thread: "Re:" now comes before the test-inbox prefix. Still to confirm on a real e-mail: whether Resend keeps our Message-ID.

The "האתר לא השיב" on Staging was our 8 s timeout against a Cloudflare site, not a mismatch.
- **Customer article e-mails** (`claude/fix-request-flow-a67639`, `f428a28`): the "הספק ביקש" e-mail removed; fix / cancelled / published in one layout, none says who asked; the admin's prefilled note no longer starts with "הספק ביקש תיקונים:"; a cancellation e-mail is now sent.
- **Real reason on "הכתבה לא הגיעה לספק"** (`claude/dispatch-error-reason-2da857`, `ececd7a`): the job's error and what to do, with a link to edit the site.
- **Sheet:** column Q "הסכם פרסום מאומת" set to "כן" for all 2,250 rows (owner: "לכל האתרים"); order 1018 then reached the supplier.
- **Checked here:** `tsc`, build, tests resend-email (45), article-reopen, supplier-thread, supplier-mail, publication-scheduling, fix-request-mail, supplier-article-request, rich-docx, delivery-reason, supplier-link-check pass.
- **Next:** credits block "קרדיט לתמונה מס׳ N:" + the exact attached file name (owner), in `supplier-mail-files`.
- **Credit per image (owner: "קרדיט לתמונה מס 1: הקרדיט ואז השם של הקובץ עצמו במדוייק")** (`lib/supplier-mail-content.ts`): `articleImages()` moved here and gives each image its number n (main, secondary, then inside the text); the attachment is `image-<n>.<ext>` and its label "תמונה מס׳ n (הראשית/נוספת/מתוך הכתבה)". The credits row lists, per image, bold "קרדיט לתמונה מס׳ n:" + the credit, and under it "קובץ: image-n.jpg"; an image too big to attach says it is in the Word file (with a download link); no credit shows "לא צוין קרדיט". Same in the plain text. Test: `tests/resend-email.mjs`. Image: notes folder `supplier-mail-files/email-credits-per-image-390.jpg`.
- **Credits per image** (`c00e1e2`, owner's format): "קרדיט לתמונה מס׳ N:" + credit, then the exact attached file name; an image too big to attach keeps its number and points to the Word file. Merged as `6d14d61`; tsc, build and the mail tests pass.
- **Supplier e-mail failed on Staging after the attachments change (owner: "עשיתי שליחה של מאמר ... והוא לא הגיע")**: reproduced locally with the exact stored text "שליחת המייל נכשלה. בדקו את החיבור ונסו שוב.": an article image that Word cannot show (WEBP here; HEIC, an unknown type or a damaged file act the same) makes `dimensions()` in `lib/rich-docx.ts` throw a plain Error while the Word file is built, before any call to Resend, and `safeFailure` turned it into the generic text. Fix: the e-mail builds the Word file in lenient mode (`articleDocx(a,{lenient:true,skipped})`): such an image, or a missing one, becomes a short note "[התמונה מצורפת למייל כקובץ נפרד]" and the image still travels as its own file; reading an image file can no longer fail the e-mail. Downloads of the Word file stay strict. `mailFailure()` stores the real cause for the admin (our Hebrew message, or "בהכנת ההודעה" + the error type, keys and addresses removed). Tests: `rich-docx` (lenient), `resend-email` (WEBP + missing image, long title: sent), `supplier-thread` (`mailFailure`). Not confirmed against the real article: check the type of its images in `files` on Staging.
- **Short subject (owner 23.11.2026, only the subject changes, the body stays)**: the supplier article e-mail subject is now only "כתבה מס׳ X לאתר <site name> (<domain>)", e.g. "כתבה מס׳ 3 לאתר מאקו (mako.co.il)". The domain comes from the site URL without "www.". X: a stock supplier gets the article's number within the stock (`supplier_sequence`); a monthly-billing supplier gets the count of articles sent to that supplier (`supplier_id` on the article or its site, any of its sites) in the same Israel-time month up to this one; a site without a supplier counts by the site. Follow-ups still answer with "Re: <same subject>" and Message-ID/References, so it stays one thread per article. Risk to know: the same subject can come back in a new month or a new stock package (e.g. "כתבה מס׳ 1 לאתר מאקו"); Gmail may group two such e-mails by subject. Tests: `supplier-thread` (stock and monthly, Israel month edge), `supplier-mail`.
- **Subject never repeats (owner 23.11.2026)**: monthly billing: "כתבה מס׳ 3 לנובמבר 2026 לאתר מאקו (mako.co.il)" (the month, Israel time, because the number restarts each month). Stock: "כתבה מס׳ 3 מתוך 10 לאתר מאקו (mako.co.il)": the stock number is the site's running delivery count (`supplier_dispatch_allocations`, unique per site), so a new package goes on (11 מתוך 20) instead of restarting; no month or order number needed. The one repeat (a slot freed by a recall and given to another article) is caught on send: if another article's first e-mail already has that subject, " · <6-letter article code>" is added. A stored thread id is used only if it is this article's own (`<supplier-article-<id>@`), so a first e-mail never references another article. Tests: `supplier-thread` (new month, new package, reused slot, foreign thread ignored, Message-ID and subject unique across 8 first e-mails), `supplier-mail`.

## 23.11.2026: categories sheet, the "חיפוש תחום" field (branch `claude/category-search-box-128dfc`)
- **Owner:** the search under "קטגוריות" looked like a thin strip glued to the title, and the page showed through the top of the popup; "a small, nice, tidy box".
- **Causes:** the sheet was 97% white with blur, so on screen the toolbar behind ("הזמנה מרוכזת", "ייצוא קטלוג") showed through around "ניקוי"; an older panel rule (`approved-customer-glass.css`, `>div:nth-child(2)`) made the title row a growing, scrolling box with a scrollbar gutter; the sheet's own scrollbar had arrows; the field text was 15px, so iPhone zooms on focus; on the phone the sheet shrank while typing, so the field moved down behind the keyboard.
- **Fix (CSS only, `app/catalog-filter-apple.css`, end of the categories part):** solid white sheet and footer, thin scrollbar without arrows, title row is a plain row again; the field is a framed box (44px phone / 40px desktop, 12px corners, light frame, blue frame and ring on focus, magnifier inside), 16px text on phones; desktop width = two of the three columns, edges on the grid lines; on phones the sheet keeps 88dvh while open and the buttons stay at the bottom. Groups, counts, footer and behaviour unchanged.
- **Checked locally (ports 8806/5200, fictional data):** desktop 960 and iPhone 390, typing "חדשות" leaves 2 categories, the field stays at the top of the phone sheet. Images: shared notes folder `category-search-box/` (before/after/after-typing, desktop + iphone). tsc and build pass.
- **Not checked:** a real iPhone keyboard (needs Staging). Seen, not touched: at 960x820 the desktop popup is taller than the space under the button, so its footer starts below the screen until you scroll the page.
- **Before upload:** nothing temporary in the repo.

## 23.11.2026: ספק שהאתר שלו לא נבדק אוטומטית → "ממתין לבדיקה ידנית" (branch `claude/link-manual-review-e423c9`)
- **Owner decision (23.11.2026):** retry in ordinary ways; if the page still cannot be read, the admin checks by hand. A wrong article, missing links, 404 or another site stay errors.
- **Check (`lib/checks.ts`, `verifyPublishedArticle` only):** the address as given (15 s), the same address again after 1.5 s, then https/http and with/without "www" (10 s each), all within 45 s (`LINK_RETRY`). Plain browser-like requests, nothing that gets around bot protection. "Could not read" = no answer, network/DNS/TLS error, 401/403/429, 5xx, or a page whose `<title>` is a bot check ("Just a moment…"). Then the result has `manual_review` + a Hebrew `review_reason` for the admin. Another spelling's 404 never replaces the cause; the same address answering 404 on the second try is final. `checkLink` (links inside a customer's article) is unchanged.
- **Flow (`lib/supplier-link-review.ts`, record `supplier-link-review:<article>`):** the submission (personal link, portal, seller, admin report) is saved as pending; the article stays `sent`, the link stays open, no customer e-mail. Admin: `POST articles/<id>/link-review` `{decision:'approve'}` → same steps as an automatic pass (published, link closed, delivery completed, customer's published e-mail, syncOrder); `{decision:'reject',note}` → the supplier sees the reason above the form on the link page and gets an e-mail in the article thread (`answer_type:'link'`: "הקישור ששלחתם לא אושר", button "שליחת קישור מתוקן"), and can send a link again.
- **Where the admin sees it:** article screen box (tag "ממתין לבדיקה ידנית", reason, link to open, "אישור הקישור" / "דחייה"), header pill; work center row "קישור ממתין לבדיקה ידנית" (content lane, task `link_review`); control-centre queue `link_review` in the publication/articles badges; article list tag. Such an article no longer counts as late at the supplier.
- **Supplier page:** "הקישור התקבל. תודה!" + "הקישור התקבל ויאושר אחרי בדיקה ידנית…", the link and three steps, no form.
- **Checked:** tsc, build; tests `supplier-link-check` (extended: retries, manual review, wrong article/404/links stay errors, approve → published flow, reject → back to supplier), resend-email, supplier-thread, supplier-mail, publication-scheduling, fix-request-mail, supplier-article-request, rich-docx, delivery-reason, article-reopen, admin-lobby-panels, payment-reports, monthly-order-lifecycle, admin-glass, admin-update-handling pass (some need the Windows path loader). `admin-content` fails as before (hidden draft). Locally on 8805/5199 with a fictional site `qa-garden-magazine.example`: submit → pending (≈2 s, DNS fails 5 times), admin approve → published + customer e-mail queued, reject → supplier sees the reason, e-mail queued.
- **Images:** shared notes folder `link-manual-review/` (supplier before/after, admin article before/after, lobby row, reject form, supplier after rejection, supplier e-mail).
- **Open:** a supplier waits up to ~45 s on a site that never answers (the page shows "בודקים את הקישור…"). If the personal link expires while pending, a rejected supplier needs a new link.
- **Before upload:** nothing temporary in the repo (the `node_modules` junction in the worktree is ignored by git).

## 23.11.2026: קופונים: מחיקה ורשימת הלקוחות שהשתמשו (branch `claude/coupons-delete-usage-20261123`, from 0e81158)
- **בקשת הבעלים:** אפשרות מחיקה ליד "השבתה", כדי שלא יצטברו קופונים. מתחת לכל קופון כפתור שנפתח לרשימה ממוספרת של הלקוחות שהשתמשו בו.
- **מה הוחלט ולמה:**
  - **מחיקה אמיתית** (`DELETE admin/coupons/:id/permanent`). השורה נמחקת מטבלת `coupons`. ההזמנות לא נוגעות בה: הקוד, ההנחה והסכומים כבר שמורים בהזמנה עצמה, ולכן סכומים, החזרים ודוחות לא משתנים. ביטול הזמנה אחרי המחיקה עובד: הוא מוריד שימוש רק מקופון שעדיין קיים (בלי שורה, לא קורה כלום). קופון שנמחק כבר לא מתקבל בסל ("הקופון אינו זמין"), כמו קופון מושבת.
  - **"השבתה" לא השתנתה.** `DELETE admin/coupons/:id` עדיין רק משבית.
  - **חסימה:** אי אפשר למחוק קופון שמשויך לחבילה פעילה, כי החבילה שולחת את הקונה לסל עם הקוד. מופיעה הודעה: להסיר אותו מהחבילה או להשבית.
  - **אישור לפני מחיקה:** חלון האישור הרגיל של המערכת. אם הקופון כבר שימש, החלון אומר בכמה הזמנות ושההזמנות נשארות כמו שהן.
  - **יומן:** כל מחיקה נרשמת ב-activity ("קופון נמחק", עם הקוד, האחוז והשימושים).
  - **מזהה הקופון בהזמנה:** הזמנה חדשה שומרת גם `coupon_id`. אם יוצרים מחדש קופון עם אותו קוד, הוא לא יורש את הלקוחות של הקופון שנמחק, וביטול של הזמנה ישנה לא מוריד לו שימוש. הזמנות ישנות שיש בהן רק קוד ממשיכות להיספר לפי הקוד.
  - **רשימת הלקוחות:** שאילתה אחת לכל הקופונים יחד, בתוך `GET admin/coupons` (JOIN של orders, coupons ו-users, בלי שאילתה לכל קופון). אותו כלל כמו המונה `uses`: הזמנה אמיתית (לא דמו) שהקוד שמור בה, בלי הזמנות שבוטלו במלואן. לכל שורה: מספור, שם, מייל, מספר הזמנה ותאריך. ריק: "עוד לא השתמשו בקופון".
- **קבצים:** `app/api/[...path]/route.ts` (GET עם רשימת השימוש, מחיקה קבועה, ביטול לפי `coupon_id`), `lib/order-create.ts` (`coupon_id` בהזמנה), `app/commerce.tsx` (`CouponCustomers`, כפתור "מחיקה" וחלון האישור), חדש: `tests/coupons-admin.mjs`.
- **עיצוב:** הכרטיס נשאר כמו שהיה. שורת "עריכת קופון" ו"השבתה" לא השתנתה. מתחתיה נוסף "מחיקה" אדום ברוחב מלא. הכפתור "לקוחות שהשתמשו (N)" נמצא מתחת לפרטי הקופון. נבדק ב-390 וב-1280, בלי גלילה לרוחב.
- **בדיקות:** `tsc` ו-build עוברים. `coupons-admin` עוברת (60 בדיקות). גם `cart-checkout-actions`, `checkout-phone-actions`, `cart-add-feedback`, `bundle-pricing`, `pricing-integration`, `split-payment`, `bundle-feedback`, `cart-export`, `n-plus-one-batch` ו-`resend-email` עוברות. `integration` (קישור ספק, שורה 74), `customer-account` (שורה 64, "כל פריטי ההזמנה כבר בוטלו") ו-`azure-workspace-actions` (ייבוא CSS ו-webp) נכשלות בדיוק כך גם ב-0e81158.
- **לא גמור או פתוח:**
  - **חלון האישור בטלפון:** הכלל המשותף `app/admin-dialogs.css:88` לחלונות אישור אדומים נותן רוחב `100vw - 24px` עם `left:0;right:0`. לכן בטלפון החלון צמוד לימין ויש רווח של 24px משמאל. זה קורה בכל חלון אישור אדום בניהול, לא רק כאן. לא תוקן, כי זה עיצוב משותף שאושר. דורש החלטה.
  - **הזמנה חודשית:** בהזמנה חודשית קיימת, קופון שהופעל ברכישה נוספת מעלה את `uses`, אבל לא נשמר בהזמנה (כך היה גם קודם). לכן הרכישה הזו לא תופיע ברשימה.
  - **ביטול חלקי:** ביטול של פריט אחד לא מחזיר שימוש (כמו קודם), ולכן ההזמנה נשארת ברשימה.
- **זמני:** אין ב-repo. השרת המקומי (8820/5220), הנתונים הבדויים והתמונות נמצאים רק ב-scratchpad.
- **השלב הבא:** הבעלים יעבור על התמונות. אחר כך: מיזוג לענף האיסוף, ופריסה ל-Staging רק באישור.

Fields 16px (no iPhone zoom), `inputmode="decimal"` on the amount, no autocorrect on the reference. Back from the confirmation on a phone does not open the keyboard by itself. Confirmation: order tag, title on one line, amount row, a new short list of what is sent (way, reference, date, file if attached), the explanation in a light blue box. Every field, check, text and the privacy note were kept.

- **Checked locally (ports 8830/5230, fictional data, orders #1322-#1324):** phone 390x844, desktop 1280, short window 390x450 with the amount focused (header, field and main button all in view). Full send: back keeps the values, the report is saved as pending; a wrong amount shows the server error in the new look. `tsc`, build, `payment-reports`, `monthly-order-lifecycle`, `cart-checkout-actions`, `checkout-phone-actions`, `cart-add-feedback` pass. `payment-closure-actions`, `customer-order-screen`, `users-payment-ui` fail the same way on the base commit (CSS imports and out-of-date mocks).

## 23.11.2026: trash for abandoned orders ("פח", owner's spec)
- **What it does:**
  - Only an abandoned order can go to the trash. That means unpaid, regular, no pending payment report, created at least `ORDER_ABANDONED_DAYS` (14) days ago, and also no open cancel request.
  - The server re-checks this with the same SQL as the "נזנחו" group (`trashableSql` in `lib/admin-order-data.ts`), inside the UPDATE itself.
  - It happens only by the admin's click. There are three places:
    - the row "…" menu
    - the order sheet footer
    - "העברת כל הנזנחות לפח (N)" on the abandoned group header (in "לתשלום" and in the lobby's "abandoned" view)
  - Every move first opens a confirmation window (title "העברה לפח…", trash-can badge, red button).
  - Restore is "שחזור" on the new "פח" tile, plus "שחזור מהפח" in the "…" menu and the sheet. Nothing is ever deleted.
- **Storage:** `trashed_at` and `trashed_by` in the order's `data` JSON, with no migration. This is the same pattern as `demo`/`on_account`; restore removes both keys. SQL helper: `liveOrderSql()` in `lib/order-trash-sql.ts`.
- **API (`lib/order-trash.ts`, wired in `app/api/[...path]/route.ts`):**
  - `POST admin/orders/trash {ids}` or `{all_abandoned:true,expected:N}`. Bulk is one request: one SELECT, then one batch with a set-based activity INSERT and a set-based UPDATE (`json_each`). It refuses if the count shown is no longer true.
  - `POST admin/orders/restore {id}`.
  - Audit lines: "הועברה לפח" / "שוחזרה מהפח".
- **Hidden everywhere except the trash view:**
  - **Admin:**
    - `adminOrderCte()`, which now excludes the trash by default, so orders, counts, export, month close, pulse and reports all follow it. Only the orders list asks for `withTrash` (bucket `trash`).
    - the lobby "bank" lane and the "abandoned" counter (`lib/admin-updates.ts`)
    - the control-center "תשלומים לבדיקה" badge
    - the admin payments badge and `payment-alerts`
    - lobby search
    - invoices list and summary (`lib/finance.ts`)
    - the customer file (`lib/account-management.ts`)
    - the users list order count
    - the reports totals
    - the content lists (`lib/admin-content.ts`)
  - **Customer:**
    - "ההזמנות שלי" and the orders counter (`me`)
    - the order page, statement, payment link and payment report all answer 404 "ההזמנה אינה זמינה"
    - article lists and the agency unpaid list
  - The admin can still open a trashed order read-only. Any action on it answers 409 until it is restored.
- **What an unpaid order holds:**
  - A split order (balance + card) reserves balance. The reservation is released while the order is in the trash (`walletAvailable`). Restore is refused if that balance was used meanwhile.
  - A real payment that arrives anyway (a card started earlier) takes the order out of the trash and logs it (`completeOrderPayment`).
  - No stock slot or writing job exists before payment (articles can't be worked until paid), so there is nothing to release.
  - A coupon use counted at checkout stays counted, because the trash is reversible. Customer cancel decrements it; trash does not. **Open question for the owner.**
- **Checked locally (fictional data):**
  - Screenshots at 390, 1280 and 1440: scratchpad `order-trash/`.
  - New test `tests/order-trash.mjs` passes (137 checks).
  - `tsc` and build pass.
  - Orders/admin/payment/customer/agency tests give the same results as on `0e81158` (same failures, same reasons).
- **Seen, not changed:** at 1280 the desktop orders list (and the opened order in "דיווחי תשלום") is cut on the left, and the action column is outside the screen. This was the same on `0e81158`. At 1440 it is fine.
- **Before upload:** nothing temporary in the repo. Local test data only: 9 orders (#1300–#1308 of the fictional customer) were aged to 20–28 days in the scratchpad database.

## 23.11.2026: card payments + Green Invoice (Morning), research only (owner asked me, no agents)
- **Already in the code:** card checkout, split balance+card, wallet top-up by card, payment link per order (`orders/<id>/payment-link`), signed callback `POST /api/payment-callback` (HMAC-SHA256 in `x-postflow-signature`, amount + ILS + event id checked, idempotent, money that can't be applied is recorded for the admin). All behind the "payments" connection (url + secret), off today (`card_enabled:false`). The protocol is our own generic one (`create_payment` → `payment_url`), so a real gateway needs an adapter.

Sandboxes exist for both. Allocation numbers (5,000₪ before VAT from 1.6.2026) are issued by Morning.

## 23.11.2026: supplier stock pool (branch `claude/supplier-stock-pool-fa49e5`, from `aca9f89`, merged `c32e744`)
- **Owner decision:** stock belongs to the supplier, for every stock supplier: one number on the supplier card, used by all its sites. A site keeps a separate stock only when the owner gives it a stock number of its own (e.g. site A 50, site B 20), not because of price. The pool number is set **only on the supplier card**; the sheet sync is unchanged (column J keeps its per-site meaning, nothing new written back). "מלאי"/"החלפה" with no number stays unlimited exactly as before, but the supplier page now shows a warning.
- **Mockup:** shared notes `supplier-stock-pool/` (`mockup-iphone-390.png`, `mockup-desktop-1280.png`, `mockup.html`). Seen by the owner, not yet explicitly approved.
- **Data:** migration `drizzle/0008_supplier_stock_pool.sql` (new table `supplier_pool_allocations`, additive). Supplier record `stock_pool` (changed only by `POST admin/supplier-pool/<id>`, stripped from supplier create/PATCH); site `stock_own` for a separate site stock. Pool packages are `stock_package` records with `supplier_id` and `pool:1`.
- **How it works:** `lib/supplier-pool.ts`. `dispatchArticle` (`lib/publication.ts`): a pool site takes the lowest free pool place (the site allocation is still written, with the pool size as its snapshot, so it is never billed monthly); the supplier e-mail number is the pool place ("כתבה מס׳ 3 מתוך 100", `supplier_sequence`/`supplier_stock_total` on the article). Ended pool → blocked with "המלאי של הספק <name> נגמר…" (`deliveryReason` fix `pool`, link to the supplier page). Cancellation before publication returns the pool place (`releaseSlotStatements`). `dispatchStockWaiting` sends waiting pool articles once there is room, and also articles whose site moved to a monthly supplier. First pool: per-site leftovers are added to the pool (default) or kept as separate site stock.
- **Screens:** supplier page block "מלאי הספק" (ok / low / ended, waiting count, separate-stock sites, sheet-word warning; "העברת אתר לספק אחר" explains the existing "צירוף אתרים" route); "הוספת מלאי" on the supplier level opens the pool dialog (no site picker; a small link opens the per-site flow); billing tab lists pool packages; lobby rows `supplier_stock` (urgent, replaces the per-article delivery rows for this reason) and `supplier_stock_low` (service, at 2 left or 10%).
- **Monthly suppliers (checked, no change needed):** `supplierSettlements` groups by the article's `supplier_id` + month, so one statement covers all the supplier's sites, each article at its own `cost_cents`, summed into one net. Covered in the new test (100+200+300 = one order of 600 for 3 sites).
- **Sheet findings (read only, gviz CSV):** no supplier has different stock numbers on its sites (only 2 numbered sites in the whole sheet, each the only site of its supplier: חדשות חיפה והקריות 38, שדרונט 6). 589 "מלאי" + 62 "החלפה" rows have no number (biggest: לייזי 436, לאוס מדיה 96, מיכאל קרויזר 39 החלפה, רשת זום 26, יוסי שושן 22 החלפה). Details: shared notes `supplier-stock-pool/findings.md`. Staging's manually added packages were not read.
- **Tests:** new `tests/supplier-stock-pool.mjs` (89 checks). tsc and build pass. Pass: per-site-billing, publication-scheduling, supplier-thread, supplier-mail, admin-glass, admin-lobby-panels, suppliers-shared-email, sheets-stock-email, sheets-two-way, month-close-sites, supplier-privacy, split-payment, payment-reports, monthly-order-lifecycle, order-trash, coupons-admin, green-invoice, the 3 Railway/Staging tests, supplier-link-check, supplier-article-request. Failing the same on the base `c32e744` (built and run separately, not caused by this work): admin-suppliers (known, 400 vs 200), admin-content ("hidden-draft stays private"), integration (supplier link title check).
- **Checked on the local server (ports 8807/5201, a scratchpad copy of local-state, fictional suppliers "אופיר (בדיקה)" pool 4 over 3 sites and "רשת אתרי U (בדיקה)" pool 10 over 4 sites):** 4 sent, 2 waiting with the supplier reason; supplier page ended/low blocks, add-stock dialog on the phone, lobby rows (urgent and service). Screenshots `built-*.png` in the shared notes folder. Seen in passing (not from this work): at 1280px the supplier sites table cuts the row "עריכה" button on the left.

## 23.11.2026: exchange suppliers built (branch `claude/exchange-suppliers-20261123`)
- **Base:** `origin/claude/supplier-stock-pool-fa49e5` (`721b0d2`, the computer's branch), then merged `claude/owner-round-20261123` (`05c1ba3`, NOTES conflict kept both sides). Nothing of either branch was overwritten.
- **Data:** migration `drizzle/0009_exchanges.sql` (additive): `exchanges` (one row per publication at an exchange site: open / claiming / taken / void / debt, the target order and site, `booked_cents`) and `exchange_adjustments` (difference booked in the month of a late choice, or a revert). Settings `exchange_default_cents` (default 5000 = ₪50) and `exchange_started_at` (set on the first sync, so every balance starts at 0).
- **How it works:** `lib/exchanges.ts`. An exchange site = sheet word "החלפה/החלפות" (`stock_mode='exchange'`). A published, valid customer article at such a site after the start opens an exchange for the supplier (sync runs in the scheduled maintenance and on the admin view) and mails "נוספה לכם החלפה". The supplier picks a site from the admin's exchange list; the take creates a ₪0 order (`payment='exchange'`, paid) for one link, with AI writing free. Cost: until a choice = the default; after a choice in the same month = the site cost; a choice after the source month ended keeps ₪50 in that month and books the difference in the month of the choice. A cancelled source before a choice voids it; after a choice it becomes a debt covered by the next publication. A cancelled target returns the exchange.
- **Kept out of other money flows:** exchange publications are never paid to the supplier and get no supplier invoice requests (`supplierPayableArticle`); exchange orders are not "missing invoice" (`lib/finance.ts`, `lib/admin-control-center.ts`); month close counts the booked exchange cost instead of the article cost and adds `exchange_adjustments` (`lib/admin-month-report.ts`, `lib/admin-month-server.ts`).
- **Screens:** supplier: tab "החלפות" instead of payments (`app/seller-exchanges.tsx`), balance, pairs, picker in the new popup look (`sky:true`), confirm sheet, no prices anywhere (server sends none; key `exchange_account` because the customer filter drops keys with "supplier"). Admin: supplier page tab "החלפות" (`app/admin-supplier-exchanges.tsx`) with tiles, rows with cost/estimate/adjustment, manual ±1 with a reason, default cost; catalog site form "זמין להחלפה" (only a site with a cost, not an exchange site).
- **API:** `GET selling/exchanges`, `GET selling/exchanges/sites`, `POST selling/exchanges/take`; admin `GET admin/exchanges?supplier=`, `POST admin/exchanges/adjust`, `GET/PATCH admin/exchanges/settings`, `POST admin/exchanges/sites`.
- **Tests:** new `tests/exchanges.mjs` (86 checks). Pass: tsc, build, the 3 Railway tests, customer-sellers-ui, seller-refinements-ui, supplier-privacy, supplier-stock-pool, supplier-mail, supplier-article-request, green-invoice, order-trash, payment-reports, split-payment, monthly-order-lifecycle, coupons-admin. Fail the same on the base (not from this work): admin-suppliers, customer-sellers, refund-supplier-ui, seller-hub-browser, seller-wide-browser, suppliers-browser, orders-finance, admin-workspace-navigation-contract, client-hub.
- **Checked on the local server** (5180, state `/home/user/qa-state-ex`, fictional supplier "sup-north" linked to qa-seller, 6 fictional sites): screenshots scratchpad `exchange/real-1-tab.png`, `real-2-pick.png`, `real-3-confirm.png`, `real-4-admin.png`.
- **Needed from the owner:** which sites open for exchanges (the "זמין להחלפה" box in the catalog); each exchange supplier needs an account linked to its supplier card.
- **Owner (23.11.2026):** admin tile "פרסם אצלו" renamed "פורסמו אצל הספק"; the picker popup shows a swap icon (ArrowLeftRight) instead of the AI sparkle (`app/dialog-badge.tsx`, any title with "החלפ"/"בתמורה" that is not a removal). Then "תפרסם".

## 23.11.2026: Pixabay next to Pexels (branch `claude/pixabay-images-20261123`, from `aa4f7f2`)
- **Why:** Pexels stopped giving new keys. Pixabay is added as a second photo library; the Pexels connection is untouched.
- **Connection:** "מאגר תמונות Pixabay" in חיבורים והגדרות (key `pixabay`, env name `PIXABAY_API_KEY`, default storage encrypted on the server), key field, "בדיקה" (one small Pixabay search). `lib/integration-security.ts`, `app/api/[...path]/route.ts` (settings list, integration list, test), `app/screens.tsx` (`connectionMeta`, `ConnectionForm`), `app/admin-system-connections.tsx` (`testable`). Photo-library windows show a picture icon (`app/dialog-badge.tsx`).
- **Hebrew / English:** Pixabay's `lang` has no Hebrew (checked in its API docs: cs, da, de, en, es, fr, id, it, hu, nl, no, pl, pt, ro, sk, fi, sv, tr, vi, th, bg, ru, el, ja, ko, zh). So: no Hebrew letters → searched as written; Hebrew → English with the "כתיבה עם AI" connection (only the Hebrew part, English kept); no AI or AI failure → a short dictionary of common topics (prefixes ה/ו/ב/ל/מ/ש/כ removed, phrases like "אוכל לכלב", "נדל"ן", "תל אביב"); no match → as written. Translations cached 30 days. The window shows "חיפשנו: dog food" with "שינוי" (searches the edited English words exactly, `exact=1`). `lib/image-search.ts`.
- **Search:** `image_type=photo`, `safesearch=true`, `orientation=horizontal`, `per_page=30`, `lang=en`. Cached 24 hours in D1 (`drizzle/0010_image_search_cache.sql`, table `image_search_cache`; old rows removed on write). Both connected: Pixabay first; Pexels when Pixabay has no results or fails. One `connection()` read per library per search, no loops over the database.
- **Chosen photo:** looked up by id (cached), downloaded from `pixabay.com` only, saved in R2 + `files` ("Pixabay-<id>.jpg"); audit "נבחרה תמונה מ-Pixabay". Credit "צילום: <photographer> · מקור: Pixabay". Saving the article stores `image_source`/`image_photographer` (and `secondary_image_*`), one activity query for both images. An expired cached download address is looked up again once.
- **Window:** `app/image-search.tsx` + `.css` (light, RTL, 2 columns on the phone, 3 on desktop, "תמונות מ-Pixabay" link as Pixabay asks). `ImageSearch` is still exported from `app/screens.tsx` (the article screen and the tests import it from there).
- **Tests:** new `tests/pixabay-search.mjs` (42 checks: English, Hebrew, mixed, no match, AI + cache + AI failure, exact words, 24h cache and expiry, Pixabay first / Pexels fallback, wrong key, import into our storage, expired address, foreign host refused, article source fields, Pexels import unchanged). Pass: tsc, build, gemini-secrets, green-invoice, resend-email, security-audit, the 3 Railway tests, exchanges. Fail the same without this change: studio-ui, studio-actions (fixture lacks `Field`), editor-import-recovery (CSS import), integration (supplier link title check).
- **Local check:** fake key + fictional cached results seeded in `/home/user/qa-state-ex` only; screenshots scratchpad `pixabay/phone-search.png`, `phone-edit.png`, `desktop-search.png`, `admin-desktop.png`, `admin-phone-dialog.png`.
- **לפני העלאה:** the migration (additive) runs on Staging start. The owner enters the real Pixabay key in חיבורים והגדרות → מאגר תמונות Pixabay and presses "בדיקה". Seen in passing (not from this work): at 1280px the "בדיקה" buttons of the connections table are cut at the left edge.

New supplier: monthly preselected. It is the default for new sites only: a site created for the supplier gets `stock_mode` 'exchange' (החלפות) or 'stock' (מלאי / מלאי משותף, the word "מלאי" without a number until a package or the pool is added); monthly adds nothing. Existing sites and the sheet's own word are not changed; a site can still be changed on its own. The shared stock amount is still added in "הוספת מלאי". `app/admin-suppliers-workspace.tsx` (`SupplierProfileForm`), `app/supplier-sky.css`, `app/api/[...path]/route.ts` (site create).
- **Exchanges:** a supplier with `billing_default` 'exchange' is an exchange supplier even before it has sites (`lib/exchanges.ts`: `exchangeSupplierOf`, `adminSupplierExchanges`).

Pass: tsc, build, supplier-stock-pool, supplier-privacy, sheets-two-way, sheets-stock-email, suppliers-shared-email, pixabay-search, the 3 Railway tests. admin-suppliers fails as before (400 vs 200, listed since c32e744).

## 23.11.2026: new "הוספת ספק" screen, spec only (owner: "אל תבצע פעולה, בוא נאפיין את כל המסך הזה מחדש")
- **Mockup:** scratchpad `new-supplier/mock-desktop.png`, `phone-1-type.png`, `phone-2-site.png`, `phone-3-more.png` (sent).

One save button: "שמירת הספק והאתרים".
- **Names (owner):** the shared amount for all the supplier's sites (was "מלאי ספק משותף") is now "מלאי · חבילה מראש"; the per-site stock (was "מלאי · חבילה מראש") is now "מלאי משתנה פר אתר", with the explanation: each site has its own stock and amount; an article uses only its own site's stock; when a site's stock ends, new articles for that site wait even if other sites of the supplier still have stock. The names change everywhere (list pills, supplier page, add-stock dialog).
- **Decisions (buttons, 23.11.2026):** prices = cost to us + list price for the customer (as in the catalog today); "מלאי · חבילה מראש": the package amount and cost are filled already on this screen and the package is recorded on save; "מלאי משתנה פר אתר": a green box in each site card with the stock amount and package cost for that site; "החלפות": the cost field is hidden in the site cards, with the line "בהחלפה לא משלמים לספק. העלות נקבעת לפי האתר שהוא בוחר בתמורה"; a URL already in the catalog → "האתר כבר בקטלוג (אצל ספק X)" with "לצרף לספק הזה" (no duplicate site).
- **Built (owner: "מאושר, לבנות"):** new screen `app/admin-supplier-new.tsx` + `.css` on `admin-supplier/new` (wired in `app/admin-suppliers-workspace.tsx`; the old `SupplierEditor` stays for existing suppliers). No new server code: saving calls `records/supplier` (with `billing_default`), then attaches catalog sites first (`admin/catalog-bulk` with the loaded pricing version), then creates new sites (`admin/websites` through `pricingApi`, so the low-price warning dialog still asks), then per-site stock (`admin/supplier-stock/<site>`) or the supplier package (`admin/supplier-pool/<id>`). A failure keeps the supplier id: saving again continues from the site that failed, without a second supplier. Logo upload `admin/catalog-logo`. URL match against `catalog?scope=admin` by host.
- **Payment cards (`BillingTypeCards`):** icon + check badge, no radio circles, the long explanation under the cards; also replaces the radio list in the details of an existing supplier. Names changed in the list pills (`billingPill`), the per-site stock dialog title and the package dialog description.
- **Checked on the local server (fictional data):** saved a supplier with "מלאי משתנה פר אתר" and two new sites + one attached catalog site, phone and desktop: sites with `stock_mode` 'stock', `stock_total` 50/20 from the packages, cost/price, logo, sample article; the attached site moved to the new supplier. Screenshots scratchpad `new-supplier/final-phone-*.png`, `real-desktop-*.png`.
- **Tests:** tsc, build, exchanges (101), supplier-stock-pool, supplier-privacy, sheets-two-way, suppliers-shared-email, pixabay-search, the 3 Railway tests pass.
- **Site cards tidied (owner: "תסדר בצורה יותר יפה את הפירוט של האתרים"):** each open card is in groups with a small heading and icon: פרטי האתר (URL + name, category + logo), מחירים לכתבה (cost + list price, with "רווח לכתבה: ₪120 (40%)" or a red loss line), מלאי לאתר הזה (green box, "יוצא ₪180 לכתבה מהחבילה"), כתבה לדוגמה. "סיום האתר" or "+ הוספת אתר" folds a complete card into a summary line (logo, name, address, chips: עלות, מחירון, מלאי, קטגוריה, or "יצורף מהקטלוג") with "עריכה" and "הסרה"; a card with something missing stays open, and saving opens the card that needs fixing. ## 23.11.2026: "העברה לפח" did nothing on the phone (on `claude/supplier-types-20261123`)
- **Cause:** on the phone an admin order opens as a sheet (`.om-sheet-layer`, z-index 95 in `orders-sky.css`), and windows open at z-index 50, so the trash confirmation (and "צירוף חשבונית" from the same sheet) opened behind it.
- **Fix (`app/admin-dialogs.css`):** while a sheet is open (`body:has(.om-sheet-layer)`), windows, menus, selects, popovers and toasts go to z-index 120. Nothing else changes.
- **Checked locally (fictional abandoned order #1007, backdated 30 days):** phone and desktop: the window opens on top, the button is reachable, the order moves to the trash (trash 1); restored and checked again on desktop. Screenshot scratchpad `trash/fixed-phone.png`. order-trash and the 3 Railway tests pass.

## 24.11.2026: PayPlus card payments, research only (owner: "תעשה כרגע רק בירור איך וכמה ותרשום לי ואז אאשר"). Nothing built.
- **Today in the code:** "סליקה ותשלומים" is a generic webhook connection. `app/api/[...path]/route.ts` (`payment-link`, ~line 536) POSTs `create_payment` (order, amount in agorot, return/cancel/callback URLs) to the connection URL with a Bearer secret and expects `payment_url` back; the provider confirms through `/api/payment-callback` (recorded as `payment_event`). Wallet top-ups by card use the same path (`lib/wallet-card.ts`).
- **PayPlus API:** REST, `https://restapi.payplus.co.il/api/v1.0/` (test: `https://restapidev.payplus.co.il/api/v1.0/`). Headers `api-key` + `secret-key`; a `payment_page_uid` from the PayPlus panel (Payment Pages). `PaymentPages/generateLink` returns a hosted payment page link (cards, bit, Apple Pay, Google Pay), with `refURL_success`/`refURL_failure` and a server callback `refURL_callback`; `more_info` comes back on the callback. The callback carries a `hash` header = HMAC-SHA256 (base64) of the body with the secret key, user-agent "PayPlus". PayPlus's own sample code is incomplete: verify against a real sandbox callback, and also confirm the transaction through the API before marking an order paid.
- **Plan if approved (one screen/flow at a time):** (1) PayPlus as a provider in the payments connection: api-key, secret-key (encrypted on the server, like Green Invoice), page UID, test/production switch, "בדיקה" button. (2) The payment link and wallet top-up call `generateLink` directly instead of the generic webhook. (3) A PayPlus callback route: check the hash, re-check the transaction with PayPlus, then the same paid flow as today (idempotent, amount must match). (4) Invoices stay with Green Invoice; PayPlus's own document issuing should be off to avoid two documents. (5) Refunds through PayPlus later, not in the first stage. Tests with a fake PayPlus server; then the PayPlus test environment on Staging only.
- **Cost (PayPlus, not official):** no public price list on payplus.co.il; sign-up described as free with no commitment. A third-party page (Shopmaster, undated): setup ₪195 once, ₪59/month up to 2,000 transactions, 0.6% PayPlus fee + about 1.2% card-company clearing. Market 2026 for comparison: 0.75%-1.6% per card transaction, monthly ₪49-149. A written quote from PayPlus is needed.
- **Needed from the owner:** a PayPlus account and a test environment (they issue test api-key, secret-key and page UID), later production keys. Keys go in "חיבורים והגדרות", never in chat.
- **Status:** waiting for the owner's approval. Nothing changed in code.

## 24.11.2026: Pixabay on Staging, previews broken and "מאגר התמונות עמוס כרגע" (owner screenshot)
- **Seen on Staging (logs):** `GET /api/image-search` 200, then `POST /api/image-import` 429 twice within a second (44ms). The search results came back (photographer names shown) but every preview was a broken image showing its tags.
- **Causes and fix (`lib/image-search.ts`, `app/api/[...path]/route.ts`):** (1) Pixabay's `webformatURL` (`pixabay.com/get/...`) opens only for the server that asked for it, so the browser could not show it. Previews now go through a new signed-in route `GET /api/image-thumb?src=` (Pixabay hosts only, jpeg/png/webp, up to 3MB, private cache 1 hour). (2) Choosing a photo made a second Pixabay request (`?id=`), which Pixabay answered with 429. The search now keeps each hit's download details in `image_search_cache` (`pixabay:id:<id>`, one `INSERT ... SELECT FROM json_each` for all hits, no loop), so choosing a photo from the results needs no Pixabay API request; the id lookup stays as a fallback. (3) A failed Pixabay request now writes its status and the start of Pixabay's answer to the server log (the key is masked), so the next failure can be read in Railway.
- **Tests:** `tests/pixabay-search.mjs` now 45 checks (previews through our server, outside host refused, sign-in required, no second request when choosing). tsc and build pass.
- **Upload (owner: "מאשר להעלות"):** Staging had moved to `claude/admin-qa-staging-20261124` (`3b5d3bf`, built on this work), so the fix was put on top of it in `claude/pixabay-fix-20261124` instead of deploying the older branch.
- **Staging deployed 24.11.2026 12:32 UTC:** Railway deployment `73bd1086` = `cb6abe4` on `claude/pixabay-fix-20261124` (= `claude/admin-qa-staging-20261124` at `92a3f8f` + the Pixabay fix + these notes). SUCCESS; "Ready on 8787", `GET /` and maintenance 200, no errors. Checked before deploying: the branch contains everything from `claude/admin-qa-staging-20261124`; only 4 files differ from `3b5d3bf` (NOTES.md, route.ts, lib/image-search.ts, tests/pixabay-search.mjs). An earlier staged change from the older branch was discarded before it went live. Next: the owner searches a photo again; if previews still fail, the server log now shows Pixabay's answer.

## 24.11.2026: Pixabay "עומס" again; previews always shown (owner: "תעשה הכל כדי שתמיד יוצגו תמונות!")
- **Cause (Staging log):** Pixabay answered "429 Rate limit exceeded". Its limit is about 100 requests a minute per key (Pixabay API docs), and this morning's fix loaded the 30 previews through our server, so every search cost 31 Pixabay requests; after 2-3 searches the key was blocked for some seconds.
- **Fix:** (1) Previews load straight in the browser from Pixabay's public image server: every 150px `previewURL` on cdn.pixabay.com has a public 640px copy (`…_150.jpg` → `…_640.jpg`; checked: 640 opens, 340 is refused). A search now costs one Pixabay request. (2) Each preview tries in turn: the 640px copy, the 150px preview, then Pixabay's own address through `/api/image-thumb`; if none opens, a quiet image placeholder shows instead of the photo's tags as text (`app/image-search.tsx` `Thumb`, `.isx-noimg`). (3) When Pixabay says busy, the server waits 2.5 seconds and asks once more; if it is still busy and Pexels is connected, Pexels results show; otherwise the screen says "מאגר התמונות עמוס לרגע. מנסים שוב אוטומטית…" and searches again by itself after 8 seconds. (4) Fewer than 8 results for a phrase of 2+ words: one wider search with fewer words is added (`pixabaySearchBroad`). Search cache key is now `pixabay:s2:` (results carry the new preview list). Files: `lib/image-search.ts`, `app/api/[...path]/route.ts`, `app/image-search.tsx`, `app/image-search.css`.
- **Tests:** `tests/pixabay-search.mjs` 48 checks (public copy first with fallbacks; busy once → asked again; busy twice → Pexels; few results → wider search). tsc, build pass.
- **Local check:** fictional cached search with real public previews: 4 tiles from the 640px copy, 1 falls back to the 150px preview, 1 shows the placeholder; 0 page errors. Screenshots scratchpad `pixabay/fix2-phone.png`, `fix2-desktop.png`. Local seed row `pixabay:s2:flower` in `/home/user/qa-state-ex` only.
- **Not pushed.** Waiting for the owner's approval. If Pixabay ever stops being enough: Unsplash (50 requests/hour in demo, 5,000/hour after Unsplash approves the app; image files do not count).

- מפתח חדש למשתמש השירות נוצר ב-Google Cloud (key id `6462cbd1…`, הקובץ הורד למחשב הבעלים). הבעלים הדביק אותו בעצמו. הישן (`68a6d0eb…`) ממשיך לשמש את Staging.

- **לא הופעל** הסנכרון האוטומטי, והכתיבה חזרה לגיליון (659 תאים) לא אושרה: מזהי הספקים באתר האמיתי (20 כרטיסים) שונים מאלה שבגיליון (מ-Staging), וכתיבה הייתה משנה את עמודת "ספק" בגיליון שגם Staging מחובר אליו. מחכה להחלטת הבעלים איזו סביבה מחוברת לגיליון.

### 24.11 (לילה, המשך) — Staging נותק מהגיליון, הסנכרון באתר האמיתי פעיל
- הבעלים: "בוא ננתק אותו מהטסטים.. אבל שישאר שם מה שכבר קיים". המפתח הישן של משתמש השירות (`68a6d0eb…`, של Staging) נמחק ב-Google Cloud. Staging שומר את כל הנתונים שלו אבל לא יכול יותר לקרוא או לכתוב בגיליון. לחיבור מחדש צריך מפתח חדש.
- לפני הניתוק Staging החזיר את שורת Greentec לגיליון; נמחקה שוב.
- התנגשויות (659): קוד הספק "בסט לינקס" (asher.megalinks@gmail.com) שונה בין הסביבות. נבחרו ערכי האתר האמיתי (נכתבו לעמודת "ספק" בגיליון). "תיירות - קרואטיה ישראל" הוחזר ל"לא" בגיליון ונעלם מהקטלוג.
- מצב: סנכרון דו־כיווני פעיל, הושלם ואושר בשיטס, 0 התנגשויות, 0 שגיאות. בקטלוג האתר האמיתי 2,662 אתרים פעילים.
- **פתוח:** קישורי הלוגו בגיליון מפנים ל-`megalinks-staging-v172…/api/catalog-logo/…`, שמחזיר 401 בלי כניסה ל-Staging. לקוחות באתר האמיתי לא יראו לוגואים. צריך להעתיק את קבצי הלוגו לאתר האמיתי ולעדכן את הקישורים.

- הבעלים: "אני רוצה שאתה תעשה את זה". נבנה ייבוא חד־פעמי (`lib/staging-import.ts`): Staging מגיש `/api/export/*` לקריאה בלבד עם `POSTFLOW_EXPORT_TOKEN` (נבדק בשער ובשרת), האתר האמיתי מושך ב-`POST /api/admin/import-staging` (מנהל בלבד, לא דורס).

## 24.11.2026 (ענן): כניסה עם Google ב-Staging

**מה נבנה:** כפתור "כניסה עם Google" במסך הכניסה של השער (`scripts/railway-staging-gateway.mjs`). התהליך: OpenID Connect עם PKCE, `state` ו-`nonce`, בעוגייה חתומה לעשר דקות. הקוד מוחלף בשרת מול Google יחד עם הסוד.
- נכנס רק חשבון שכבר מוגדר בשער (`POSTFLOW_STAGING_ACCOUNTS` / `..._CUSTOMER_ACCOUNTS`), ורק עם מייל ש-Google אימתה: Gmail, או דומיין Workspace (`hd`). מייל אחר מקבל הודעה ונשאר בחוץ.
- לקוח נשאר מחובר 30 יום. מנהל (בעל המערכת ו-`POSTFLOW_ADMIN_EMAILS`) נשאר 8 שעות.
- הכניסה עם סיסמה נשארה כמו שהייתה.
- מופעל רק כש-`GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` ו-`GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` מוגדרים ב-Railway (`scripts/railway-staging-start.mjs`). האתר האמיתי לא מעביר אותם, ולכן שם אין שינוי.
- כתובת החזרה שצריכה להיות רשומה ב-Google: `https://megalinks-staging-v172.up.railway.app/__staging/google/callback`.
- בדיקה: `tests/railway-gateway-google.mjs` (Google מדומה, בלי רשת).

**לא נבנה עדיין:** פתיחת חשבון חדש למי שלא מוגדר, כולל מסך אישור תנאים, ושינוי באתר האמיתי.

## 25.11.2026 (ענן): הרשמה וכניסה עם Google לכל אחד, ב-Staging (ענף `claude/google-signup-20261125`)

**מה הוחלט:** כל אחד יכול להירשם עם Google (החלטת הבעלים). הכניסה נשארת בשער (`scripts/railway-staging-gateway.mjs`), שכבר מחזיק את ה-session. החשבונות עצמם נפתחים ומתחברים באתר (`lib/google-account.ts`).
- **מסך הכניסה** לפי ההדמיה (`?s=login`), Google בלבד: `scripts/google-signup-pages.mjs`. טופס הסיסמה של חשבונות הבדיקה עבר ל-`/__staging/password`, עם קישור קטן בתחתית.
- **לקוח חדש:** אחרי Google מגיע למסך התנאים (`/__staging/google/terms`, לפי `?s=terms`). שום דבר לא נוצר עד האישור. האישור נבדק בשרת. נשמרים: רשומת `terms:<user>` (מועד, גרסת תקנון ופרטיות, הנוסח, דרך הכניסה), קישור `identity:google:<sub>`, ושורה ביומן. דיוור רק אם סומן (`setMarketing(...,'signup')`), עם הנוסח הקיים `MARKETING_WORDING` במקום הנוסח של ההדמיה (כמו שהתוכנית דרשה).
- **לקוח קיים:** מזוהה לפי המייל ש-Google אימתה, ומחובר לחשבון הקיים עם ההזמנות שלו. בכניסה הבאה מזוהה לפי `sub`.
- **אבטחה:** רק מייל ש-Google אימתה, ורק Gmail או דומיין Workspace. מייל של מנהל מקבל הרשאת מנהל רק כשהוא מאומת כך. הקריאות מהשער לאתר עוברות רק בכתובת הפנימית, עם מפתח האוטומציה.
- **תוקף:** לקוח 30 יום, מנהל 8 שעות.
- **פתוח בלי כניסה** (כדי ש"לצפייה במחירון בלי להירשם" והקישורים לתקנון יעבדו): דף הבית, המחירון, המסמכים המשפטיים, המדריכים, הקבצים הסטטיים וקריאה מה-API. הבקשות האלה מגיעות לאתר בלי זהות ובלי עוגיות. כל השאר, וכל כתיבה, דורשים כניסה.
- כל זה פעיל רק כשמוגדרים ב-Railway `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` ו-`GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET`, ומפתח האוטומציה מועבר לשער (`scripts/railway-staging-start.mjs`). האתר האמיתי לא מעביר אותם, ולכן שם אין שינוי.
- בדיקות: `tests/railway-gateway-signup.mjs`, `tests/railway-gateway-google.mjs`, `tests/railway-staging-gateway.mjs`. האתר נבדק גם מקומית מול מסד בדיקה (נתונים פיקטיביים).

**לא נבנה:** שאלה מחדש כשגרסת התנאים משתנה; לקוחות קיימים לא רואים את מסך התנאים; עדכון המסמכים המשפטיים (תקנון 5.2, פרטיות, עוגיות) שמתארים את הכניסה עם Google; ביטול sessions כשמשנים תפקיד למשתמש.

## 25.11.2026: מסך הכניסה והתנאים בעיצוב חדש (ענף `claude/login-split-build-20261125`, מ-`b89b039`)

**מה הוחלט:** הבעלים פסל את ההדמיה המפוצלת של הענן ("לא אותו דבר כמו שהוא עשה") ואישר אחרי כמה סבבים את `design/login-screen/mockup-system2.html`:
- רקע לבן אחד. מימין הכניסה, משמאל כותרת "כתבות וקישורים באתרים הגדולים בישראל", משפט על מערכת הזמנות למקדמי אתרים ולבעלי אתרים, ו"מאגר עצום של 2,500+ אתרים".
- ארבעה כרטיסים עם לוגו אמיתי: ווינט, ישראל היום, כלכליסט, מעריב. **בלי מחירים** (הבעלים: המחירים משתנים). מאחוריהם עומק: אור רך, נקודות ושורת כרטיסים מטושטשת.
- הטקסט מתמקד בקנייה. **לא לכתוב שבעלי אתרים יכולים להוסיף את האתר שלהם** (הבעלים).
- מתחת לכפתור: "ההרשמה בחינם. יאללה, תרשמו אותי!" (נוסח של הבעלים).
- בטלפון: כותרת גדולה, ארבעה לוגואים, שורת המאגר מתחתם, בלי ההתראות. כפתור Google נראה בלי גלילה.

**קבצים:** `scripts/google-signup-pages.mjs` (רק העיצוב; ההתנהגות, הטופס, `MARKETING_WORDING` ו-`TERMS_LINE` לא השתנו), `public/brand/login-logo-*.png` (הלוגואים מהמחירון, אותו מקור).
**נבדק:** שלוש בדיקות השער עברו (37, 22, 61). המסכים צולמו מהקוד עצמו עם ה-CSP של השער: כל התמונות והגופנים נטענים, אין גלילה לצד, הכפתור "פתיחת החשבון" נדלק רק אחרי סימון התנאים, שורת השגיאה מופיעה.
**לא נעשה:** push ופריסה ל-Staging, ממתין לאישור הבעלים.
**לדעת:** הלוגו של ישראל היום מציג רק "היום". מהדמיות שלא אושרו לא נשמר כלום ב-git.

## 26.11.2026 night: bulk order redesign built (branch `claude/bulk-order-build-20261126`, local only)
- **From:** `claude/bulk-order-redesign-20261125` (design files, owner approved), merged with `claude/notes-dialog-20261125` so it includes what Staging runs (`7abd56a`). Does **not** include the catalog-window fixes of `claude/catalog-popovers-20261126` (separate branch, also local).
- **Template** (`lib/bulk-template.ts`): built per user in the browser. Sheets הזמנה (first, RTL, frozen headers, hidden "סוג שורה", orange banner + example row with "דוגמה:" in each cell, lists for site / content method / customer, whole words 100–5,000, red words cell for professional writing without words), איך ממלאים, אתרים (customer price), הלקוחות שלי. File name "הזמנה מרוכזת - מגה לינקס.xlsx". Opened in the owner's Excel without repair (validations, hidden column checked).
- **Reading** (`lib/bulk-order.ts`): finds the header row, skips rows marked in "סוג שורה", keeps old files ("מילים לכתיבה בתשלום" = professional writing) and CSV. New rules: content method required to be one of the list; professional writing 100–5,000 words; AI needs topic, keyword or link; AI refused when not configured. `bulkSuggestSite` for "אולי התכוונתם".
- **Page** (`app/bulk-order-view.tsx`, `app/bulk-order-page.css`): per `design/bulk-order/mockup.html`. Old `bulk-instructions.tsx` and `bulk-order-approved.css` removed. `GET /api/bulk-options` says whether AI is configured.
- **AI after payment** (`app/api/[...path]/route.ts`): `validBrief` keeps `auto_ai`; `autoAiDrafts` runs from the every-minute maintenance tick (2 per tick) for workable orders, claims each article once (`auto_ai_started`), skips text already written, one email per order (`email:ai-drafts:<order>`). The article's "generate" and the tick share `prepareAiDraft`.
- **Checked locally (fictional data):** tests bulk-order, bulk-template (new), bulk-auto-ai (new, source wiring), cart-add-feedback, seo, catalog-grid; customer-glass-actions bulk part passes (its last workspace check fails on Windows line endings, as before). Browser flow: template download → filled with openpyxl → upload → old-format file → CSV with errors → add to cart: the cart items carry auto_ai / editorial words as chosen. Screenshots: `design/bulk-order-build/`.
- **Not tested:** the AI drafting itself (no AI key locally) and its email; run once on Staging with a paid test order.
- **לפני העלאה:** the screenshots showed AI as available by faking `/api/bulk-options` in the screenshot script only; nothing in the code fakes it.
- **Next:** owner approval for push and Staging.

## 26.11.2026 evening: fixes on top of the cloud's catalog windows, and a homepage sentence (local commit, not pushed)
- **Header vanished with "סינון ומיון" open (owner's screenshot):** the dialog's scroll lock sets `overflow:hidden` on body, and with `html{overflow:clip}` body becomes the sticky header's scroll box, so the header left with the page. Fix: `body:has(.catalog-sort-dialog.is-anchored[data-state=open]){overflow:visible!important}` (computer only); the dialog still blocks page scrolling. Checked at 1440×900, 1280×720 and phone 390: header stays at 0 with both windows.
- **Sort buttons back as they were (owner):** מומלץ, מחיר ↑, מחיר ↓, DR; "DR מהנמוך" removed with them.
- **Window footer** is solid white on the computer (the cards showed through behind "איפוס סינונים").
- **Homepage (owner approved the mockup):** under the headline, "מאגר של 2,700+ מהאתרים החזקים ביותר בישראל, מערכת מתקדמת לניהול ומעקב, והכל במקום אחד." (`home/index.html` `.hero__sub`, Heebo ML 500; `lib/home-page-html.ts` and `lib/pr-page-html.ts` regenerated). Mockups: `design/homepage-hero/`.
- **Checked:** tsc, build, seo.mjs, catalog-home-layout, catalog-list-filter, catalog-grid, cart-add-feedback pass. seo-audit.mjs fails the same before this change; cart-checkout-actions, checkout-phone-actions and catalog-home-actions do not run on Windows (ESM path), Railway runs them.
